                                                        Slow Cooked Beef Brisket

                                                        Slow-cooked beef brisket braised in a blended spiced onion-tomato sauce with smoked paprika, cajun seasoning and warm spices.

                                                        Brisket

                                                        • 1.2 kg brisket, with some fat
                                                        • 1 tsp salt
                                                        • 1 tsp freshly ground black pepper

                                                        Sauce

                                                        • 4 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 4 small red onions, roughly chopped
                                                        • 1 tsp salt, or to taste
                                                        • 10 medium garlic cloves, sliced
                                                        • 1 medium red bell pepper, chopped
                                                        • 1 medium tomato, chopped
                                                        • 1 inch cinnamon stick
                                                        • 1 medium bay leaf
                                                        • 2 tsp smoked paprika
                                                        • 1–1.5 tsp freshly ground black pepper
                                                        • 1.5 tsp cajun seasoning
                                                        • 1 tsp oregano
                                                        • 1 tsp thyme
                                                        • 1 tsp freshly ground coriander
                                                        • 1 tsp freshly ground cumin
                                                        • 1 vegetable or beef stock cube
                                                        • 1 litre water

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Coat the brisket in the salt and pepper, rubbing in thoroughly.
                                                        2. Sear all sides in a very hot pan, 3–4 minutes per side, until deeply browned; set aside.
                                                        3. In the same pan, add the onions, garlic and salt with a splash of water; sauté over medium-low heat, deglazing as you go.
                                                        4. Add the bell pepper and tomato; cook until softening.
                                                        5. Add the herbs, spices and a splash of water; cook 5 minutes over medium-low, stirring, until the raw spice smell is gone.
                                                        6. Crumble in the stock cube, pour in 1 litre water, bring to a boil over medium-high heat and boil 5 minutes.
                                                        7. Cool slightly, then blend into a smooth sauce.
                                                        8. Return the brisket to the pan (or an oven-safe deep dish), pour the sauce over and around it, and cover tightly with baking paper plus a lid or foil — no steam should escape.
                                                        9. Heat the oven on its highest setting for 30 minutes, then place the brisket inside and reduce to 170°C.

                                                        The reel continues in the comments with the final braising time; the caption method above is complete up to the oven step.

                                                        Restaurant-Style Spinach Artichoke Dip

                                                        https://www.chelseasmessyapron.com/spinach-artichoke-dip/

                                                        A cheesy, creamy baked spinach artichoke dip based on a restaurant favourite — best served with warm, crispy pita chips.

                                                        Ingredients

                                                        • 1 (8-ounce) bag fresh baby spinach, coarsely chopped
                                                        • 1 (8-ounce) package cream cheese, softened
                                                        • 1 tbsp lemon juice (optional)
                                                        • 1/2 cup mayo
                                                        • 1/4 cup sour cream
                                                        • 1 cup shredded sharp cheddar cheese
                                                        • 1 (14-ounce) can artichoke hearts, drained and chopped
                                                        • 1 tsp garlic powder
                                                        • 1/4 tsp salt
                                                        • 1/4 tsp pepper
                                                        • 1/2 cup grated parmesan cheese

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 350°F.
                                                        2. In a large bowl, combine everything except the parmesan; stir until well mixed.
                                                        3. Spread into an 8×8-inch baking pan or 2-quart casserole dish; sprinkle parmesan evenly on top.
                                                        4. Bake 35 minutes, until hot and bubbly.
                                                        5. Broil 1–2 minutes until golden on top, watching closely. Serve warm with pita chips, tortilla chips, toasted baguette or veggies.

                                                        The reel's twist: serve with warm, crispy pita chips only — 'it changed the way I eat spinach artichoke dip.' Lighten it with light cream cheese, mayo and sour cream.

                                                        Chimichurri Chicken with Garlic Parm Mash

                                                        Chimichurri-brushed chicken thighs with garlic parmesan mashed potatoes, asparagus and garlic bread — a full dinner spread (4 servings).

                                                        Chimichurri Chicken

                                                        • 1 lb skinless chicken thighs
                                                        • 1 tbsp chicken bouillon
                                                        • Paprika, chili powder, onion powder, garlic powder, oregano, salt, pepper
                                                        • 1 tsp olive oil
                                                        • Asparagus
                                                        • Roasted vegetable seasoning

                                                        Chimichurri

                                                        • 1 bunch parsley
                                                        • 1 bunch cilantro
                                                        • 2 tbsp diced white onion
                                                        • 3 garlic cloves
                                                        • 1/4 cup olive oil
                                                        • 2 tbsp red wine vinegar
                                                        • 1/2 tsp salt
                                                        • 1/2 tsp pepper
                                                        • 1/2 tsp oregano
                                                        • 1/2 tsp red pepper flakes

                                                        Garlic Parmesan Mashed Potatoes

                                                        • 2 large russet or gold potatoes
                                                        • 1/2 cup heavy cream or milk
                                                        • 1 tbsp butter
                                                        • Grated parmesan
                                                        • Garlic powder, salt, pepper

                                                        Garlic Bread

                                                        • 2 tbsp softened butter
                                                        • 1 tsp parsley
                                                        • 1 tsp lemon juice
                                                        • 2 tsp parmesan
                                                        • 1 tsp minced garlic
                                                        • 1/8 tsp cayenne
                                                        • 1/8 tsp red pepper flakes
                                                        • 1/8 tsp salt
                                                        • 1/8 tsp pepper
                                                        • Ciabatta bread

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Season the chicken thighs with bouillon, paprika, chili powder, onion powder, garlic powder, oregano, salt, pepper and olive oil; sear in cast iron over medium-high heat 3–4 minutes per side, brushing with chimichurri while cooking.
                                                        2. Remove the chicken; add asparagus to the pan, season with roasted vegetable seasoning and cook 5–7 minutes until soft.
                                                        3. Chimichurri: blend or finely chop everything together.
                                                        4. Mash: peel and dice the potatoes, boil in salted water until fork-tender, drain and mash with the cream, butter, parmesan, garlic powder, salt and pepper.
                                                        5. Garlic bread: mix the butter with parsley, lemon juice, parmesan, garlic, cayenne, red pepper flakes, salt and pepper; brush on ciabatta and bake at 400°F for 5–7 minutes.
                                                        6. Assemble: garlic parm mash, chimichurri chicken thighs, asparagus and garlic bread; top with extra chimichurri.

                                                        Best-Ever Hot Chocolate

                                                        A rich stovetop hot chocolate with milk, cream, cocoa, chocolate chips and sweetened condensed milk.

                                                        Ingredients

                                                        • 3 cups milk
                                                        • 1 cup heavy whipping cream
                                                        • 1/2 cup cocoa powder
                                                        • 1/2 cup chocolate chips
                                                        • 1 (14 oz) can sweetened condensed milk
                                                        • 1/2 tsp vanilla
                                                        • 1/2 tsp salt

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Combine the milk and cream and heat until hot but not boiling.
                                                        2. In a separate bowl, combine the cocoa powder and chocolate chips; add some of the hot milk and mix until all the chocolate is melted.
                                                        3. Add the chocolate back to the pan with the hot milk, then add the sweetened condensed milk, vanilla and salt.

                                                        Chicken Raita with Tandoori Chicken

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A twist on the classic: smoky tandoori-spiced chicken folded into a tadka-topped raita with garlic, onion and coriander — great with parathas, biryani or BBQ platters.

                                                        Tandoori Chicken

                                                        • 200 g boneless chicken
                                                        • 1 tsp red chilli powder
                                                        • 1 tsp chaat masala
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garam masala
                                                        • 1 tsp tandoori masala
                                                        • 1 tbsp lemon juice
                                                        • 1 tbsp ginger-garlic paste
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • Oil, to cook

                                                        Raita (Dahi Mix)

                                                        • 1 cup thick yogurt
                                                        • 1 tsp black salt
                                                        • 1 tsp roasted cumin powder
                                                        • 3–4 garlic cloves, crushed
                                                        • 1 onion, chopped
                                                        • 1 green chilli, finely chopped
                                                        • Fresh coriander, chopped

                                                        Tadka

                                                        • 3 tbsp mustard oil
                                                        • 1/4 tsp cumin seeds
                                                        • 1/2 tsp red chilli powder
                                                        • 1/4 tsp turmeric powder
                                                        • 1 tsp kasoori methi

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Mix the chicken with all the tandoori marinade ingredients; rest 15–20 minutes.
                                                        2. Cook in a little oil on medium flame until juicy and slightly charred; cool completely.
                                                        3. Slice the chicken into small pieces; add the onion, green chilli and coriander; set aside.
                                                        4. Whisk the yogurt with black salt, roasted cumin and crushed garlic.
                                                        5. Heat the mustard oil till smoky; crackle the cumin seeds, then add red chilli powder, turmeric and kasoori methi; switch off the flame and pour into the yogurt; mix well.
                                                        6. Add the seasoned yogurt to the chicken mixture and mix until everything is coated.
                                                        7. Garnish with coriander; serve with parathas, biryani, BBQ platters, or as a snack on its own.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Camembert Pull-Apart Bread

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A melty Camembert baked in the center of garlic-dill pull-apart rolls — cut into the cheese and dip.

                                                        Dough

                                                        • 330 g all-purpose flour (T55)
                                                        • 1 egg
                                                        • 100 g lukewarm milk
                                                        • 80 g lukewarm water
                                                        • 7 g dry yeast
                                                        • 40 g melted butter
                                                        • 1 tbsp honey
                                                        • 1 tbsp milk powder
                                                        • 1 tsp salt

                                                        Assembly & Finish

                                                        • 1 whole Camembert
                                                        • Mozzarella, for filling
                                                        • 1 egg yolk, for brushing
                                                        • Melted butter
                                                        • Fresh dill, chopped
                                                        • Crushed berries, for topping
                                                        • A sprig of thyme

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Mix all the dough ingredients and knead until smooth; let rise until doubled.
                                                        2. Place the whole Camembert in the center of a round baking dish.
                                                        3. Cut the dough into small squares; add mozzarella to the center of each and pinch to seal into balls.
                                                        4. Arrange the dough balls around the Camembert; brush with egg yolk.
                                                        5. Bake at 180°C (350°F) for about 20 minutes, until golden.
                                                        6. Mix melted butter with chopped dill and brush over the hot rolls.
                                                        7. Serve warm — pull the rolls and dip them in the melted Camembert.

                                                        Creme Fraiche Chicken Pot Pie

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        An elegant, elevated chicken pot pie: golden lattice crust over a vermouth, creme fraiche and Dijon filling with leeks, parsnip and mushrooms.

                                                        Filling

                                                        • 3 chicken legs
                                                        • 1 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 leek, chopped
                                                        • 1 parsnip, chopped
                                                        • 1 potato, chopped
                                                        • 1 cup mushrooms, sliced
                                                        • 2 tbsp chopped tarragon
                                                        • 1/2 cup vermouth
                                                        • 1/2 cup all-purpose flour
                                                        • 3 cups chicken stock
                                                        • 1/4 cup creme fraiche
                                                        • 2 tbsp Dijon mustard
                                                        • 1 cup frozen peas

                                                        Crust & Finish

                                                        • 1 disc pie dough
                                                        • 1 egg, beaten
                                                        • Salt and pepper
                                                        • Extra creme fraiche and chopped chives, to serve

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 425°F. Sear the chicken legs in olive oil in a braiser or oven-safe pot, 4–5 minutes per side; transfer to a baking sheet and roast 20–30 minutes until cooked through.
                                                        2. To the same pot, add the leek, parsnip, potato and mushrooms; cook 8–10 minutes until softened.
                                                        3. Add the tarragon and vermouth; let the alcohol cook off, then add the flour and cook 1–2 minutes.
                                                        4. Slowly stir in the chicken stock, creme fraiche and Dijon; simmer until thickened; add the peas.
                                                        5. Shred the chicken and add it to the filling with any juices from the tray; let cool slightly.
                                                        6. Roll out the pie dough and cut 8–10 strips about 1 inch wide; form a lattice over the filling.
                                                        7. Brush with egg, sprinkle with salt and pepper, and bake ~30 minutes until golden. Serve with extra creme fraiche and chives.
                                                        View reel

                                                        French Onion Spinach Chicken Pasta Bake

                                                        https://www.ambitiouskitchen.com/spinach-chicken-pasta-bake/

                                                        A protein-packed healthy chicken pasta bake with two full bags of spinach and a creamy, lightened-up parmesan sauce — no heavy cream involved.

                                                        Pasta

                                                        • 10 oz dry pasta shells (or rotini, fusilli, or gluten-free)

                                                        Chicken

                                                        • 1 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 lb boneless skinless chicken breast, diced into 1/2-inch cubes
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garlic powder
                                                        • Salt and freshly ground black pepper

                                                        Caramelized Onions & Spinach

                                                        • 1/2 tbsp olive oil (or butter)
                                                        • 2 large yellow onions, sliced
                                                        • Salt and freshly ground black pepper
                                                        • 10–12 oz fresh spinach (2 x 5-oz bags)

                                                        Parmesan Cheese Sauce

                                                        • 3 tbsp salted butter
                                                        • 1/4 cup all-purpose flour (or whole wheat or gluten-free)
                                                        • 2 cups 2% or whole milk (or unsweetened almond milk)
                                                        • 1/2 cup grated parmesan cheese
                                                        • 1 tsp kosher salt
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garlic powder
                                                        • Freshly ground black pepper

                                                        Topping

                                                        • 1/2 cup panko bread crumbs (gluten-free if desired)
                                                        • 1 tbsp salted butter

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 350°F; spray a 2-quart baking dish or 11x7-inch pan with nonstick spray.
                                                        2. Boil the noodles until al dente; drain and set aside.
                                                        3. Cook the diced chicken in olive oil over medium-high heat, seasoned with garlic powder, salt and pepper, 5–7 minutes until no longer pink; transfer to a bowl.
                                                        4. In the same pot, cook the sliced onions with olive oil or butter, salt and pepper, stirring occasionally until completely caramelized and golden brown (add a splash of water or white wine if sticking); reduce the heat and stir in the spinach until wilted, about 2 minutes. Add to the bowl with the chicken.
                                                        5. Make the sauce in the same pot: melt the butter, whisk in the flour a little at a time, alternating with the milk and whisking out lumps; bring to a boil, then simmer until thickened. Off the heat, stir in the parmesan, garlic powder, salt and lots of black pepper.
                                                        6. Stir the cooked noodles, chicken, spinach and caramelized onions into the sauce; season with salt and pepper and pour into the prepared pan.
                                                        7. Mix the bread crumbs with the melted butter, sprinkle over the top, and bake 25 minutes until golden brown. Serves 6.

                                                        Easy Red Thai Curry

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A cozy, warming red Thai curry with chicken, baby corn, bell peppers and spinach in a coconut milk sauce.

                                                        Main

                                                        • Chicken breasts, cut into cubes
                                                        • 1 tbsp fish sauce (or oyster sauce)
                                                        • 2 tbsp red Thai curry paste
                                                        • 1/2 onion, finely chopped
                                                        • 2 cloves garlic, minced
                                                        • 1 tbsp ginger, minced
                                                        • 1 red bell pepper, sliced
                                                        • Baby corn, halved
                                                        • 1 can (400ml) coconut milk
                                                        • Juice of 1/2 lime
                                                        • 1–2 tsp brown sugar (to taste)
                                                        • Fresh basil and coriander, to garnish
                                                        • Cooked rice, to serve
                                                        • Baby spinach

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Cook the cubed chicken in a hot pan or wok; set aside.
                                                        2. Add the red Thai curry paste, onion, garlic and ginger; sauté until fragrant.
                                                        3. Add the red bell pepper and baby corn.
                                                        4. Pour in the coconut milk; simmer 5–10 minutes.
                                                        5. Add the spinach and simmer 2–3 minutes more.
                                                        6. Stir in the fish sauce, lime juice and brown sugar.
                                                        7. Serve over cooked rice, garnished with fresh basil and coriander.

                                                        Butter Chicken (Kals Cooking Pot)

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Takeaway-style butter chicken with a charred, marinated chicken folded into a rich bhuna tomato masala — cream and methi finish per the ingredient list.

                                                        Chicken Marinade (1 hour)

                                                        • 6–7 chicken thighs
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • 2 tbsp Greek yogurt
                                                        • 1 tbsp ginger garlic paste
                                                        • 1 tbsp tandoori masala
                                                        • 1 tbsp kasuri methi (dried fenugreek leaves)
                                                        • 1/2 lemon, juiced

                                                        Masala

                                                        • 3–4 tbsp ghee
                                                        • 1 tbsp cumin seeds
                                                        • 1 tbsp mustard seeds
                                                        • 2–3 bay leaves
                                                        • 4–5 cardamom pods
                                                        • 3–4 cloves
                                                        • 1 tsp chilli flakes
                                                        • 2 tbsp ginger garlic paste
                                                        • 2–3 green chillies
                                                        • 2 medium onions, finely chopped
                                                        • 1 tbsp tandoori masala
                                                        • 1 tbsp kasuri methi
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp turmeric
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp cumin powder
                                                        • 2 tbsp tomato purée
                                                        • 1 cup passata
                                                        • 30g butter
                                                        • Splash of double cream

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Marinate the chicken thighs with salt, yogurt, ginger garlic paste, tandoori masala, kasuri methi and lemon juice; rest 1 hour (or overnight).
                                                        2. Cook the marinated chicken in the air fryer at 190°C for 15–18 minutes until charred and cooked through; set aside.
                                                        3. Heat the ghee over medium-low; add the whole spices and cook ~20 seconds until aromatic.
                                                        4. Add the ginger, garlic and green chillies; sauté ~1 minute.
                                                        5. Add the onions and cook on medium-low 10–15 minutes until soft and golden, adding splashes of water at intervals.
                                                        6. Add the spices, tomato purée and passata; mix well.
                                                        7. Add the butter and bhun the masala over medium heat 15–20 minutes until the oil separates, adding splashes of water at intervals.
                                                        8. Add the cooked chicken with the cream and kasuri methi; simmer to marry; serve hot.

                                                        Perfect Cheesecake

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        The creator's 4th batch this week — a silky, no-crack cheesecake with a buttery graham crust, baked low and slow with a water bath.

                                                        Graham Cracker Crust

                                                        • 2 sleeves (18 sheets) graham crackers, crushed to crumbs (about 2 1/2 cups)
                                                        • 1/2 cup (1 stick) salted butter, melted

                                                        Cheesecake Batter

                                                        • 32 oz full-fat cream cheese (4 x 8 oz blocks), room temperature
                                                        • 1 cup (200g) sugar
                                                        • 1/2 tsp salt
                                                        • 2 tsp vanilla extract
                                                        • 2 tbsp all-purpose flour
                                                        • 4 large eggs, room temperature
                                                        • 1/2 cup sour cream (120g), room temperature
                                                        • 1/2 cup heavy cream (120g), room temperature

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Crush the graham crackers, mix with the melted butter, and press firmly into a springform pan; chill or bake briefly to set.
                                                        2. Mix the cream cheese with the sugar, salt, vanilla and flour until smooth, scraping the bowl.
                                                        3. Add the eggs one at a time, mixing on low just until incorporated.
                                                        4. Mix in the sour cream and heavy cream until just combined — do not overmix.
                                                        5. Pour the batter over the crust and place the pan in a water bath.
                                                        6. Bake at 325°F for 60–70 minutes, until the edges are set but the center still jiggles slightly.
                                                        7. Cool in the turned-off oven, then chill at least 4 hours (overnight is best) before slicing.

                                                        Cajun Chicken & Wedges Tray Bake

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A no-fuss tray bake: crispy cajun-spiced chicken legs and wedges roasted with peppers, onion and red pepper sauce.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 4 chicken legs
                                                        • 4–5 large potatoes, cut into wedges
                                                        • 2 bell peppers, cut into chunks
                                                        • 1 large onion, quartered
                                                        • 2 tbsp Cajun seasoning
                                                        • 1 tbsp paprika
                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 2 tbsp red pepper sauce
                                                        • 1 tsp garlic granules
                                                        • Salt to taste

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 200°C (fan).
                                                        2. Toss the chicken legs and potato wedges with the Cajun seasoning, paprika, garlic granules, olive oil and salt on a large tray.
                                                        3. Add the peppers and onion to the tray.
                                                        4. Roast for 35–40 minutes until the chicken is cooked through and the wedges are crispy, turning once.
                                                        5. Drizzle with the red pepper sauce before serving.

                                                        Easy Overnight Buns (Dinner Rolls)

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Soft, fluffy overnight buns mixed the night before and baked fresh in the morning — cold-proofed for up to 36 hours.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 550 g wheat flour
                                                        • 20 g fresh yeast (or 7 g dry)
                                                        • 10 g salt
                                                        • 10 g sugar
                                                        • 1 egg
                                                        • 1.5 dl milk
                                                        • 1.5 dl water
                                                        • 50 g butter

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Melt the butter in the warm milk and water; whisk in the egg.
                                                        2. Add the yeast and sugar; stir to dissolve.
                                                        3. Add the flour and salt; knead 8–10 minutes until smooth and elastic.
                                                        4. Cover and place in the refrigerator overnight (12–36 hours).
                                                        5. The next morning, shape into buns and place on a lined tray.
                                                        6. Let rise at room temperature until puffy, about 45–60 minutes.
                                                        7. Bake at 220°C for 10–12 minutes until golden.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Dilli Wala Chicken Korma

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Old-Delhi style chicken korma: charred, blended onions in mustard oil and ghee with the creator's 'royal korma' masala.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 2 tbsp mustard oil
                                                        • 2 tbsp ghee
                                                        • 3 big onions, sliced (charred then blended into a paste)
                                                        • 2 big green chillies
                                                        • 1 kg chicken
                                                        • 2 big tomatoes, blended
                                                        • 1 tbsp salt
                                                        • 1 tbsp red chilli powder
                                                        • 1 tsp turmeric powder
                                                        • 2 tbsp coriander powder
                                                        • 1 tsp cumin powder
                                                        • 1 tsp chicken masala
                                                        • 2 bay leaves
                                                        • 2 black cardamom
                                                        • 2 green cardamom
                                                        • 1 inch cinnamon stick
                                                        • 1 tsp cumin seeds
                                                        • 1 cup water
                                                        • 250 g curd, whisked (dahi)
                                                        • 1 tsp garam masala
                                                        • 1 tsp royal korma masala
                                                        • Coriander, to garnish

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Heat the mustard oil and ghee in a heavy pot.
                                                        2. Char the sliced onions in the hot oil until deeply golden; blend into a smooth paste.
                                                        3. Return the onion paste to the pot with the green chillies; cook a few minutes.
                                                        4. Add the chicken and brown it in the onion masala.
                                                        5. Add the blended tomatoes and all the powdered spices, whole spices and salt; cook until the oil separates.
                                                        6. Add the water and the whisked curd; stir and simmer on low until the chicken is tender and the gravy is thick.
                                                        7. Finish with garam masala and royal korma masala; garnish with coriander.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Masala Chicken Traybake

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A one-tray masala chicken dinner with baby potatoes, peppers, cherry tomatoes and onion — serve with rice, warmed wraps or flatbreads.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 800 g chicken thighs, cut into large pieces
                                                        • 1 tbsp crushed ginger
                                                        • 1 tbsp crushed garlic
                                                        • 2 tbsp neutral oil
                                                        • 1 tbsp malt vinegar
                                                        • 1 tsp ground turmeric
                                                        • 1 tbsp garam masala
                                                        • 2 tsp mild curry powder
                                                        • 1 tsp red chilli powder
                                                        • 1 tsp smoked paprika
                                                        • 1 tsp ground cumin
                                                        • Salt and pepper
                                                        • 600 g baby potatoes, quartered
                                                        • 2 bell peppers, cut into chunks
                                                        • 8–10 cherry tomatoes
                                                        • 1 large red onion, cut into wedges
                                                        • Fresh coriander, to serve

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 200°C (fan 180°C).
                                                        2. Toss the chicken with the ginger, garlic, oil, vinegar and all the spices, salt and pepper; marinate 15 minutes (or longer).
                                                        3. Spread the marinated chicken on a large tray with the potatoes, peppers, tomatoes and onion.
                                                        4. Roast 40–45 minutes, turning once, until the chicken is cooked through and everything is charred at the edges.
                                                        5. Scatter over fresh coriander and serve with rice, warmed wraps or flatbreads.

                                                        Korean Crispy Chicken & Scallion Pancake (Padakjeon)

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Crispy fried chicken served over a golden scallion pancake (padakjeon) — the ultimate crispy-creamy combo.

                                                        Chicken Marinade (1 hour)

                                                        • 1 kg chicken breast or thighs, cut into bite-size pieces
                                                        • 1 tbsp maida (all-purpose flour)
                                                        • 1 tbsp corn flour
                                                        • 1/2 tsp salt
                                                        • 1/2 tsp black pepper
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garlic powder
                                                        • 1 egg

                                                        Crispy Coating

                                                        • 1/2 cup maida
                                                        • 1/4 cup corn flour
                                                        • 1/2 tsp salt
                                                        • 1/2 tsp black pepper
                                                        • Oil, for frying

                                                        Scallion Pancake

                                                        • 1 cup maida
                                                        • 1/4 cup corn flour
                                                        • 1 tsp salt
                                                        • 1 cup water
                                                        • Lots of spring onions (scallions), chopped
                                                        • Oil, for shallow frying

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Marinate the chicken with the maida, corn flour, salt, black pepper, garlic powder and egg; rest 1 hour.
                                                        2. Make the pancake batter: whisk the maida, corn flour, salt and water until smooth; stir in the chopped spring onions.
                                                        3. Shallow-fry the batter in a hot pan to make one large pancake until golden and crispy on both sides; remove.
                                                        4. Dredge the marinated chicken in the crispy coating mixture and fry until golden and crisp.
                                                        5. Serve the crispy chicken over the scallion pancake, cut into wedges.

                                                        Paneer Methi Malai

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A Diwali-special creamy paneer gravy with fresh methi (sautéed to take off the bitterness), cashew-tomato gravy and peas.

                                                        Gravy Base

                                                        • 1/4 cup ghee or butter
                                                        • 2 tsp oil
                                                        • 1 bay leaf
                                                        • 2 cardamom pods
                                                        • 2 cloves
                                                        • 1 tsp cumin seeds
                                                        • 1/2 stick cinnamon
                                                        • 1 onion, roughly chopped
                                                        • 2 green chilies, roughly chopped
                                                        • 4 cloves garlic, roughly chopped
                                                        • 1/2-inch ginger nub
                                                        • 1 large tomato, roughly chopped
                                                        • 1/4 cup cashews

                                                        Spices & Finish

                                                        • 2 tsp turmeric
                                                        • 3 tsp garam masala
                                                        • 3 tsp ground coriander
                                                        • 2 tsp red chili powder
                                                        • 2 tsp ground cumin
                                                        • 1/3 cup heavy cream
                                                        • 1/3 cup frozen peas (boiled)
                                                        • 1 bunch methi (fenugreek leaves), chopped and rinsed
                                                        • 2 tsp dried fenugreek (kasuri methi)
                                                        • 1 cup grated paneer
                                                        • 1 tsp sugar
                                                        • Salt to taste

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. In a pan, heat oil; add the whole spices and cumin seeds and let them splutter.
                                                        2. Add the onion, ginger, garlic and green chilies; sauté until the raw smell leaves and the onions are translucent.
                                                        3. Add the tomatoes and cashews; cover and cook until soft; let cool, then grind to a smooth gravy.
                                                        4. Separately, sauté the chopped methi in a little oil (this removes its bitterness); set aside.
                                                        5. In the same pan, add butter and oil; on low heat cook the turmeric, red chili powder, garam masala and cumin-coriander powders for 1 minute without burning.
                                                        6. Add the prepared gravy carefully; stir and add salt to taste.
                                                        7. Add the cooked methi, boiled peas, sugar and cream; warm through.
                                                        8. Serve topped with grated paneer.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Marry Me Chocolate Mousse Cake

                                                        https://bakewithzoha.com/marshmallow-mousse/

                                                        The creator's most viral cake: moist chocolate cake layered with fluffy marshmallow mousse, dark chocolate fudge and milk chocolate ganache. Bake one day, fill and frost the next.

                                                        Chocolate Cake

                                                        • 2 cups all-purpose flour (240g)
                                                        • 2 cups granulated sugar (400g)
                                                        • 3/4 cup Dutch-processed cocoa powder (63g)
                                                        • 1 tsp espresso powder (optional)
                                                        • 2 tsp baking powder
                                                        • 1 1/2 tsp baking soda
                                                        • 1 tsp kosher salt
                                                        • 1 cup buttermilk (240g), room temperature
                                                        • 3/4 cup neutral oil (180g)
                                                        • 1 cup hot water (240g)
                                                        • 2 eggs + 1 yolk, room temperature
                                                        • 2 tsp vanilla extract

                                                        Marshmallow Mousse

                                                        • 2 1/2 tsp unflavored gelatin
                                                        • 2 tbsp cold water
                                                        • 2 1/2 cups heavy cream, cold
                                                        • 1 tsp vanilla extract
                                                        • 4 oz marshmallow fluff (1 cup)
                                                        • 1/4 cup confectioners' sugar

                                                        Dark Chocolate Fudge

                                                        • 8 oz dark chocolate, roughly chopped (do not use chips)
                                                        • 2/3 cup heavy cream
                                                        • 1 tbsp light corn syrup (optional)

                                                        Milk Chocolate Ganache

                                                        • 8 oz milk chocolate, finely chopped (do not use chips)
                                                        • 2/3 cup heavy cream

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Whisk the dry ingredients in a large bowl.
                                                        2. Whisk the buttermilk, oil, eggs, yolk and vanilla in a jug; stir into the dry ingredients.
                                                        3. Add the hot water and whisk until just smooth.
                                                        4. Pour into two 8-inch lined pans; bake at 350°F (180°C) for 20–30 minutes. Cool 20 minutes in the pans, then fully on a rack.
                                                        5. Bloom the gelatin in cold water 5 minutes. Beat the cream with the vanilla to soft peaks; mix in the marshmallow fluff and sugar; melt the gelatin and beat it in. Chill 1 hour.
                                                        6. Melt the fudge ingredients together; cool to a spreadable consistency. Melt the ganache ingredients; cool slightly.
                                                        7. Layer: cake, fudge, mousse, cake; coat the cake in ganache. Chill, slice and serve.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Chicken Sisig

                                                        https://www.iankewks.com/chicken-sisig-recipe/

                                                        A weeknight chicken sisig using store-bought rotisserie chicken for the same sizzling, tangy flavor as the classic pork version in a fraction of the time. The key: cook the chicken long enough that it flakes and browns so it isn't clumpy or soggy.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 2 tbsp unsalted butter
                                                        • 1 small red onion, chopped
                                                        • 5 garlic cloves, minced
                                                        • 1–2 red Thai chilies, minced
                                                        • 2 cups shredded cooked chicken (rotisserie)
                                                        • 1 tbsp calamansi juice (or lemon/lime)
                                                        • 2 tsp liquid seasoning (or soy sauce)
                                                        • 2 tsp oyster sauce
                                                        • 1/2 tsp ground black pepper

                                                        Topping

                                                        • 1 fried sunny side-up egg (or cracked fresh onto a sizzling plate)
                                                        • 2 tbsp chopped red onions
                                                        • 2 tbsp crushed chicharron
                                                        • 1 tbsp chopped green onion

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. In a medium pan over medium-high heat, melt the butter and sauté the onion 2–3 minutes until softened.
                                                        2. Add the garlic and chilies; sauté 2 more minutes until the garlic is golden.
                                                        3. Add the chicken and cook 5–7 minutes, stirring, until flaky, browned and no longer moist.
                                                        4. Pour in the calamansi juice, liquid seasoning, oyster sauce and black pepper; stir-fry 1–2 minutes to season evenly.
                                                        5. Transfer to a sizzling plate (greased with ~1 tbsp butter) or a serving plate; top with the egg, red onions, chicharron and green onion. Optional: stir 2 tbsp mayonnaise into the chicken for extra richness.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Bitter Gourd Fry (Karela Fry)

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Simple and tasty bitter gourd fry: salted, squeezed karela fried golden, then tossed with a mustard-jeera-onion tempering and finished with lemon and coriander.

                                                        Bitter Gourd

                                                        • Bitter gourd (karela), cut into slices
                                                        • Salt
                                                        • 2 tsp oil

                                                        Tempering

                                                        • 1 tsp mustard seeds
                                                        • 1 tsp cumin (jeera)
                                                        • Hing (asafoetida)
                                                        • Curry leaves
                                                        • 1 onion, sliced
                                                        • 1/2 tsp turmeric
                                                        • 1 tsp red chilli powder
                                                        • Coriander (daniya) powder
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • Lemon juice
                                                        • Coriander leaves

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Toss the sliced karela with salt and set aside 30 minutes; squeeze out the water and discard it.
                                                        2. Heat 2 tsp oil and fry the karela until golden; remove and set aside.
                                                        3. In the same pan, temper mustard seeds, jeera, hing and curry leaves; sauté the sliced onion.
                                                        4. Add the fried karela back in and mix.
                                                        5. Add turmeric, red chilli powder, salt and daniya powder; sauté 2 minutes.
                                                        6. Finish with lemon juice and coriander leaves; serve hot.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Hyderabadi Chicken Dum Biryani

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Oven-dum Hyderabadi chicken biryani for a true shaadi-wala taste: overnight-marinated chicken layered with 70%-cooked basmati, sealed tight and baked — 'no shortcuts, no store-bought masala.'

                                                        Marination (overnight best)

                                                        • 1 kg chicken, bone-in medium pieces
                                                        • 3 tbsp ginger garlic paste
                                                        • 1 tbsp red chili powder
                                                        • 1/4 tsp turmeric
                                                        • 1.5 tbsp freshly ground garam masala (or biryani masala)
                                                        • 1 cinnamon stick
                                                        • 6 green cardamoms
                                                        • 4 cloves
                                                        • 1 tbsp shah jeera
                                                        • 2 cups yogurt, whisked
                                                        • 2 large onions, thinly sliced and fried golden (1½ in marinade, ½ for layering)
                                                        • 1/2 cup oil (from the fried onions)
                                                        • 2 tbsp ghee
                                                        • 1/2 bunch cilantro, chopped
                                                        • 3 sprigs mint, chopped
                                                        • 3–4 green chilies, crushed
                                                        • Salt to taste

                                                        Rice (parboil to 70%)

                                                        • 5 cups basmati rice, soaked 30 minutes
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp shah jeera
                                                        • 2–3 green cardamoms
                                                        • 1½ tbsp salt (the water should taste slightly salty)

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Mix all marination ingredients with the chicken; cover and marinate at least 4 hours, ideally overnight.
                                                        2. Boil the soaked rice in salted water with shah jeera and cardamom until 70% cooked; drain.
                                                        3. In a large oven-safe pot, spread the marinated chicken evenly and layer the parboiled rice over it.
                                                        4. Top with fried onions, cilantro, mint, green chilies and a drizzle of ghee (plus optional saffron milk).
                                                        5. Cover tightly with foil plus the lid to trap steam.
                                                        6. Bake at 500°F for 30 minutes, then reduce to 350°F and bake 45 minutes more.
                                                        7. Rest 10–15 minutes before opening; serve.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Creamy Coconut Sriracha Meatballs

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Quick and easy chicken meatballs loaded with ginger, garlic and sriracha, finished in a creamy coconut sauce with lime — serves 4.

                                                        Meatballs

                                                        • 600g chicken breasts
                                                        • 4 spring onions, roughly chopped
                                                        • 2 cloves garlic
                                                        • 1 inch ginger, roughly chopped
                                                        • A handful of coriander leaves and stalks
                                                        • 2 tbsp sriracha
                                                        • 2 tbsp fish sauce
                                                        • 1 medium egg
                                                        • 40g breadcrumbs
                                                        • 1 tbsp neutral oil

                                                        Sauce

                                                        • 1 x 400g tin coconut milk
                                                        • 1 tbsp fish sauce
                                                        • 1 tbsp sriracha
                                                        • Juice of 1/2 lime
                                                        • A small handful finely chopped coriander leaves
                                                        • Smashed peanuts and chilli oil, to garnish

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Blitz everything for the meatballs except the oil in a food processor until smooth; wet your hands and roll into 16 meatballs.
                                                        2. Heat the oil in a large non-stick pan over medium-high; cook the meatballs 3–4 minutes on two sides until golden.
                                                        3. Add the coconut milk, fish sauce, sriracha and lime juice; bring to a boil and cook 4 minutes.
                                                        4. Turn the meatballs in the sauce and cook 4–5 minutes more, until the chicken is cooked through and the sauce has thickened.
                                                        5. Scatter over coriander and serve immediately with peanuts and chilli oil.

                                                        Lasagna Soup

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        All the cozy layers of lasagna — rich sauce, noodles, melty ricotta — made in one pot in a fraction of the time.

                                                        Soup Base

                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 lb lean ground beef (or Italian sausage, or a combo)
                                                        • 1 yellow onion, finely diced
                                                        • 2 medium carrots, finely diced
                                                        • 3 ribs celery, finely diced
                                                        • 6–8 cloves garlic, minced
                                                        • 3 tbsp tomato paste
                                                        • 1/2 cup dry red wine (optional)
                                                        • 1 (28 oz) can San Marzano tomatoes, crushed by hand (or 24 oz marinara)
                                                        • 6–8 cups low-sodium beef broth
                                                        • 1 Parmesan rind (optional)
                                                        • 2 bay leaves
                                                        • 1 sprig fresh thyme
                                                        • 1 tsp dried oregano
                                                        • 1 tsp dried basil
                                                        • 1 tsp dried parsley
                                                        • 1 tsp dried thyme
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp Calabrian chilies or red pepper flakes
                                                        • 1/2 cup heavy cream (or 1/4 cup cream + 1/4 cup mascarpone), room temperature
                                                        • 1/2 lb lasagna noodles, broken into bite-size pieces

                                                        Cheese Mixture

                                                        • 1 cup ricotta
                                                        • 1/2 cup freshly grated Parmesan
                                                        • 1 cup shredded mozzarella
                                                        • 1/4 cup chopped fresh parsley
                                                        • Pinch of nutmeg (optional)
                                                        • Zest of 1/4 lemon (optional)
                                                        • Salt and black pepper

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Brown the beef in olive oil with the onion, carrot, celery and garlic until softened.
                                                        2. Stir in the tomato paste and red wine (if using); add the crushed tomatoes, broth, Parmesan rind, bay leaves, thyme and dried herbs.
                                                        3. Simmer until the vegetables are tender and the broth is rich.
                                                        4. Add the broken lasagna noodles and cook until al dente (use more broth for a soupier pot).
                                                        5. Stir in the heavy cream off the heat.
                                                        6. Ladle into bowls and top each with a generous dollop of the cheese mixture.

                                                        Method assembled from the ingredient list; the creator's full method is linked in their bio.

                                                        Boneless Chicken White Karahi

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A creamy white karahi — whole spices bloomed in butter, chicken simmered in yogurt and finished with fresh cream and lemon.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 700g boneless chicken
                                                        • 1 tsp cumin seed
                                                        • 2 bay leaves
                                                        • 6–7 peppercorns
                                                        • 4 cloves
                                                        • 2 medium onions
                                                        • 7–8 garlic cloves
                                                        • 1 inch ginger
                                                        • 1 tsp salt
                                                        • 1 tsp black pepper
                                                        • 1 tsp coriander powder
                                                        • 1/2 cup yogurt
                                                        • 2 tbsp fresh cream
                                                        • Butter or ghee, for cooking
                                                        • Lemon juice
                                                        • Julienned ginger and coriander, to garnish

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Grind the onion, garlic and ginger into a fine paste.
                                                        2. In a pan, heat butter; add cumin, bay leaves, peppercorns, cloves and the chicken, and fry until the chicken changes colour.
                                                        3. Add the onion paste and all the spices; mix well.
                                                        4. Add the yogurt; mix and cook on low heat 10–12 minutes or until the chicken is cooked.
                                                        5. Stir in the fresh cream and a squeeze of lemon juice.
                                                        6. Garnish with julienned ginger and coriander; serve with roti or naan.

                                                        Spicy Changezi Aloo Sabzi

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Changezi-style spicy potato curry for two: mustard oil, whole and ground spices, a creamy milk-water gravy and amchur for tang.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 1 tsp oil
                                                        • 1 tsp mustard oil
                                                        • 1/2 tsp ajwain
                                                        • 1/2 tsp cumin seeds
                                                        • 1 tsp coriander seeds
                                                        • 1/2 inch cinnamon stick
                                                        • 1/2 tsp ginger paste
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garlic paste
                                                        • 1 onion, paste
                                                        • 1 tsp Kashmiri red chili powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp turmeric
                                                        • 1/2 tsp coriander powder
                                                        • 1 tsp cumin powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garam masala
                                                        • 1/4 tsp dry ginger powder
                                                        • 1/4 tsp fennel seeds
                                                        • 1/4 tsp nutmeg powder
                                                        • 1/4 tsp kasuri methi
                                                        • 1/4 cup tomato puree

                                                        • 1/2 cup water

                                                        Dark Chocolate Caramel Cookies

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Bakery-style cookies with melty caramel, gooey chocolate and a crisp edge — an easy one-bowl, no-chill recipe.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 3/4 cup melted butter (170g)
                                                        • 1 1/4 cups brown sugar (250g)
                                                        • 2 large eggs
                                                        • 2 tsp vanilla extract
                                                        • 2 cups all-purpose flour (250g)
                                                        • 1 tsp baking powder
                                                        • 1 tsp baking soda
                                                        • 3/4 tsp salt
                                                        • 3/4 cup rolled oats (60g)
                                                        • 1 cup chocolate chips + 1/2 cup dark chocolate chunks
                                                        • 1/2 cup caramel bits

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Whisk the melted butter, brown sugar, eggs and vanilla until smooth.
                                                        2. Add the flour, baking powder, baking soda and salt; stir until nearly combined.
                                                        3. Fold in the oats, chocolate chips, dark chocolate chunks and caramel bits.
                                                        4. Scoop generous balls onto a lined baking tray.
                                                        5. Bake at 350°F (175°C) for 10–12 minutes, until edges are golden but centers look slightly underdone.
                                                        6. Cool on the tray 5 minutes before moving — they firm up as they cool.
