                                                        Ultimate Chicken Biryani

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Dahi-based chicken biryani cooked on dum under a layer of fluffy basmati, finished with saffron milk and birista — 'garma garam biryani ka swad, bina jyada mehnat.'

                                                        Chicken & Marinade

                                                        • 1 kg chicken (10–12 pieces)
                                                        • 1 cup curd (dahi)
                                                        • 4 tbsp ginger-garlic paste
                                                        • 1 tbsp red chilli powder
                                                        • 1 tsp turmeric
                                                        • 1 tsp salt (to taste)
                                                        • 1 tsp roasted jeera powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp coriander powder
                                                        • 2 tbsp garam masala

                                                        Rice & Dum Layering

                                                        • 3 cups basmati rice (soaked 30 minutes), boiled 90% with whole spices, 2 tbsp oil, 2 tbsp ghee, lemon juice and salt
                                                        • 2 tbsp fried onions (birista)
                                                        • 2 tbsp fresh coriander, chopped
                                                        • 2 tbsp fresh mint, chopped
                                                        • 2–3 green chillies, slit
                                                        • 1 tsp saffron in 1/4 cup warm milk
                                                        • 1 tsp kewra water
                                                        • 2 tbsp ghee, for finishing

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Mix the chicken with curd and all the marinade spices; rest 20–30 minutes.
                                                        2. Cook the marinated chicken 10 minutes on medium-high, then reduce to low and cook 10 minutes more until tender and the masala coats the chicken.
                                                        3. Layer the cooked chicken at the bottom of a handi.
                                                        4. Add a layer of the 90%-cooked rice; sprinkle birista, coriander, mint, green chillies, saffron milk and kewra water.
                                                        5. Drizzle ghee over the top and cover tightly.
                                                        6. Place on dum (very low heat) for 20–25 minutes.
                                                        7. Rest 10 minutes before opening; serve hot.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Nawabi Malai Chicken

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A creamy, white nawabi-style chicken gravy — cashew-melon seed richness with just a hint of warmth. For best results, don't compromise on butter, cream and milk.

                                                        Main

                                                        • Butter + oil, for cooking
                                                        • 2 onions, roughly chopped
                                                        • 1 tbsp ginger-garlic paste
                                                        • 1.5 tsp cumin seeds
                                                        • 4–5 green cardamom
                                                        • 5–6 cloves
                                                        • 10 cashews + 10 melon seeds (magajtari), soaked in 1/2 cup hot water
                                                        • 750g boneless chicken
                                                        • 1/2 cup thick curd (dahi)
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • 1/2 cup milk
                                                        • 1/2 tsp pepper powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garam masala
                                                        • 1/4 tsp nutmeg powder
                                                        • 1/4 cup fresh cream
                                                        • Kasoori methi + coriander, to garnish

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. In a pan, melt butter with a little oil; fry the onions until golden brown.
                                                        2. Add the ginger-garlic paste, cumin, cardamom, cloves and the soaked cashews and melon seeds; fry 2–3 minutes.
                                                        3. Let cool, then grind to a smooth paste with a splash of water.
                                                        4. In the same pan, melt more butter and cook the chicken with salt 8–10 minutes until the edges are golden.
                                                        5. Add the ground paste and cook until the oil separates.
                                                        6. Whisk the curd and add it; simmer 5–6 minutes.
                                                        7. Add the milk, pepper powder, garam masala and nutmeg.
                                                        8. Finish with fresh cream and garnish with kasoori methi and coriander.
                                                        View reel

                                                        • Olive oil, for the pan

                                                        Chicken Tawa Masala

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Crispy-edged tawa masala chicken with ginger juliennes, green chillies and a finishing masala of kasuri methi and crushed black pepper.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 500g chicken, medium pieces
                                                        • 1/2 cup curd (dahi)
                                                        • 1 tbsp ginger garlic paste
                                                        • 1 tbsp lemon juice
                                                        • 2 tsp Kashmiri red chilli powder
                                                        • 1 tsp coriander powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp turmeric
                                                        • 1/2 tsp cumin powder
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • 3–4 tbsp mustard oil (or ghee)
                                                        • 1 onion, finely chopped
                                                        • 1 tsp crushed coriander seeds
                                                        • 1/2 tsp crushed black pepper
                                                        • 1/2 tsp kasuri methi
                                                        • Ginger juliennes and green chillies, to garnish
                                                        • Fresh coriander, to garnish

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Marinate the chicken with curd, ginger garlic paste, lemon juice, all the ground spices and salt; rest 30 minutes.
                                                        2. Heat the mustard oil on a tawa or heavy pan until it just starts to smoke; reduce the heat.
                                                        3. Add the marinated chicken and cook 6–7 minutes until the masala starts to stick and the edges crisp.
                                                        4. Add the chopped onion and cook 2–3 minutes more until golden.
                                                        5. Sprinkle over the crushed coriander seeds, black pepper and kasuri methi; toss.
                                                        6. Garnish with ginger juliennes, green chillies and coriander; serve hot with roti or naan.

                                                        Dubai Trending Iffa Chicken

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Dubai's viral-style Iffa chicken: a spiced, marinated chicken platter finished in a creamy masala gravy — best served with khubz or naan.

                                                        Marination (1 hour)

                                                        • 1 kg chicken
                                                        • 2 tbsp curd (dahi)
                                                        • 2 tbsp ginger garlic paste
                                                        • 1.5 tbsp Kashmiri red chilli powder
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp black pepper powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp cumin powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp coriander powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp tandoori masala
                                                        • 1 tbsp garam masala
                                                        • 1 tbsp meat masala
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • 2 tbsp lemon juice
                                                        • 3 tbsp oil

                                                        Masala

                                                        • 3 tbsp oil
                                                        • 2 onions, sliced
                                                        • 1 tbsp ginger garlic paste
                                                        • 2 tomatoes, chopped
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp black pepper powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp Kashmiri red chilli powder
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp cumin powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp tandoori masala
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp garam masala
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • 1/2 cup curd (dahi)
                                                        • 1 cup fresh cream
                                                        • Coriander leaves, to garnish

                                                        Meatball Sub Casserole

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A cozy meatball sub casserole with a garlic-butter Italian bread base, marinara, mozzarella and Parmesan — ready in about 30 minutes.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 1 loaf Italian bread, cubed (about 6 cups)
                                                        • 1/2 cup unsalted butter, melted
                                                        • 4 cloves garlic, minced
                                                        • 1 tbsp Italian seasoning
                                                        • 28 oz frozen meatballs, thawed
                                                        • 24 oz marinara sauce
                                                        • 2 cups shredded mozzarella cheese, divided
                                                        • 1/2 cup freshly grated Parmesan cheese, divided
                                                        • 1/4 cup fresh basil or parsley, chopped

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 375°F (190°C) and grease a 9x13-inch baking dish.
                                                        2. Toss the bread cubes with the melted butter, garlic and Italian seasoning; spread in the baking dish.
                                                        3. Nestle the meatballs among the bread cubes and pour the marinara over everything.
                                                        4. Sprinkle 1 cup mozzarella and 1/4 cup Parmesan over the top.
                                                        5. Bake 15 minutes, until the cheese is melted and bubbly.
                                                        6. Sprinkle with the remaining mozzarella and Parmesan; bake 10 more minutes until golden.
                                                        7. Garnish with fresh basil or parsley; let rest 5 minutes before serving.

                                                        Black Pepper Chicken

                                                        https://iamneverfull.com/black-pepper-chicken/

                                                        A quick stir-fry of chicken and sweet peppers in a bold black pepper sauce — serve with fluffy white rice and maybe an egg on top.

                                                        Main

                                                        • 2 large chicken breasts, cut into 1-inch pieces
                                                        • Salt and pepper
                                                        • 1 tsp ground ginger
                                                        • 2 tbsp cornstarch
                                                        • 2 tbsp cooking oil, divided
                                                        • 1 bell pepper, cut into 1-inch pieces
                                                        • 1 onion, cut into 1-inch pieces
                                                        • 2 cloves garlic, minced

                                                        Sauce

                                                        • 2 tsp ground black pepper
                                                        • 1 tsp dark brown sugar
                                                        • 3 tbsp soy sauce
                                                        • 1/2 tbsp rice vinegar
                                                        • 1 tbsp cooking wine or dry sherry
                                                        • 3 tbsp water
                                                        • 1 tbsp cornstarch

                                                        Steps

                                                        One Pot Marry Me Chicken (Tuscan Chicken)

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A creamy, flavoursome one pot meal with sundried tomatoes, basil and parmesan - pizza flavours in a chicken dinner that serves 3-4.

                                                        Seasoning

                                                        • 1 tsp smoked paprika
                                                        • 1 tsp oregano
                                                        • 1 tsp thyme
                                                        • 1 tsp garlic granules
                                                        • Salt & black pepper to taste

                                                        Chicken & sauce

                                                        • 600g chicken breasts
                                                        • 1 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 large onion, diced
                                                        • 4 tsp minced garlic
                                                        • 70g sundried tomatoes, chopped
                                                        • 1 tbsp tomato puree
                                                        • 300ml low sodium chicken stock
                                                        • 150g creme fraiche
                                                        • 50g grated parmesan
                                                        • Handful of fresh basil, chopped
                                                        • 0.5-1 tsp chilli flakes (optional)

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Season the chicken with the smoked paprika, oregano, thyme, garlic granules, salt & black pepper.
                                                        2. In a hot pan, add olive oil and cook the chicken on both sides till almost cooked through, then remove & set aside.
                                                        3. In the same pan, add a little more oil if needed, then fry the onions for a couple of minutes to soften.
                                                        4. Add the minced garlic, chopped sundried tomatoes & tomato puree & stir, cooking for another 2 mins.
                                                        5. Stir in the chicken stock & cook for 5 mins before adding the creme fraiche & mixing till smooth.
                                                        6. Mix in the chopped basil & grated parmesan before adding the chicken & juices back in.
                                                        7. Cover the chicken with sauce & cook through for a few mins to finish.
                                                        8. Serve with pasta, potatoes & greens or brown rice & enjoy!

                                                        Best served with pasta, potatoes or rice.

                                                        Korean Comfort Soup (Instant Pot)

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A warming Korean-style beef, bean sprout and radish soup made in the Instant Pot - ready to heat up when the family comes home hungry from a trip.

                                                        Soup

                                                        • 1 lb beef (cut of your choice - stew meat or shabu meat work well)
                                                        • 2 tbsp neutral oil (e.g. avocado oil)
                                                        • 1/2 tsp - 2 tbsp Gochugaru (adjust to your liking)
                                                        • 1 tbsp sesame oil
                                                        • 2 tbsp minced garlic
                                                        • 4 tbsp soy sauce for soup
                                                        • 5 scallion stalks, chopped into 2-inch long pieces
                                                        • 1 bag bean sprouts
                                                        • 1/2 medium Korean radish, cut into small thin pieces
                                                        • 6 cups water
                                                        • 3 seafood stock coins
                                                        • 1 tsp fish sauce (optional)

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Prep your veggies.
                                                        2. Press the SAUTE function on the Instant Pot and saute the meat until halfway cooked.
                                                        3. Add the radish pieces, soy sauce for soup, sesame oil, minced garlic and Gochugaru and saute everything together for 3 minutes.
                                                        4. Add 6 cups of water, the bean sprouts and scallions. If you have fish sauce, add 1 tsp here (optional).
                                                        5. Close the lid and cook on the SOUP setting (10 minutes, high pressure).
                                                        6. When done, quick release and add salt and pepper to your liking. Serve with rice and enjoy!

                                                        Gochugaru amount is adjustable - use as little as 1/2 tsp or up to 2 tbsp depending on spice preference.

                                                        Spicy Cheese Korean Chicken

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A one-pot Korean-style chicken marinated in gochujang and finished with melted mozzarella — sweet, spicy, and tangy. Serve with noodles or egg fried rice.

                                                        Ingredients

                                                        • 300 gm chicken, cubed
                                                        • 1 tsp chili flakes
                                                        • 2 tsp garlic powder
                                                        • 2 tsp pepper powder
                                                        • 1/2 tbs red chili powder (creator used Kashmiri red chili powder)
                                                        • 1 tbs gochujang paste
                                                        • 2 tbs Korean sauce (e.g. Heinz Korean sauce, or any spicy chili or Sriracha sauce)
                                                        • 1 tbs soya sauce
                                                        • 1/2 tbs vinegar
                                                        • 1 tbs honey
                                                        • Salt
                                                        • 3–4 tbs mozzarella
                                                        • 1 tbs spring onion

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Except for the mozzarella and spring onion, mix all the ingredients well to a paste, then add the chicken and marinate it well. Adjust spice levels to your taste.
                                                        2. Transfer the chicken to a fry pan, close with a lid, and cook on medium heat — be sure not to burn the chicken.
                                                        3. Once the chicken is almost cooked, add the mozzarella, close with the lid, and allow it to melt.
                                                        4. Garnish with spring onions and serve with noodles or egg fried rice.

                                                        Gochujang is available in most leading supermarkets; substitute regular chili paste if unavailable (it will be spicier). DIY gochujang substitute: grind red chilies with tamarind, vinegar, red chili powder, and chicken stock.

                                                        Crispy Egg Salad Toast

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Egg salad amped up by pan-crisping it until golden, served on toast with mashed avocado — a fun twist on classic egg salad. Original recipe idea by healthyishfoods.

                                                        Ingredients

                                                        • 4 soft-boiled jammy eggs, chopped
                                                        • 1/4 cup shredded swiss cheese
                                                        • 1/4 cup grated parmesan
                                                        • 2 tbsp chives, chopped
                                                        • 1 tbsp sriracha
                                                        • 1 tsp paprika
                                                        • 2 tbsp avocado oil
                                                        • 1/2 large avocado
                                                        • Sourdough bread

                                                        Steps

                                                        3. Add 1/4 cup of egg salad mixture to the skillet and sear until golden brown and crispy, about 3 minutes on each side.
                                                        4. Assemble the toast: top toasted bread with mashed avocado, crispy egg salad, and some extra paprika and chives.

                                                        Kerala-Style Prawn Biryani

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A Kerala-style prawn biryani with coconut milk, short-grain jeeraga rice, and crispy microwave onions — serves 4 (~390 kcal per serving).

                                                        For the marinade

                                                        • 400g cleaned and deveined prawns
                                                        • 1 tsp ginger-garlic paste
                                                        • 1 tsp red chili powder
                                                        • 1/4 tsp turmeric powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp black pepper powder
                                                        • 1 tsp coriander powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garam masala
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • Juice of 1/2 lemon

                                                        For the masala

                                                        • 1 tsp coconut oil
                                                        • 1 tsp ghee
                                                        • 2 green cardamom pods
                                                        • 1 bay leaf
                                                        • 2 large onions, thinly sliced
                                                        • 1 green chili, chopped
                                                        • 2 small tomatoes, thinly sliced
                                                        • 1/4 tsp each of chili powder, coriander powder, garam masala (optional — same as marinade)
                                                        • 1/3 cup thick coconut milk
                                                        • Pinch of sugar (optional)
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • Fresh coriander leaves, chopped

                                                        For layering

                                                        • 1.5 cups short-grain rice (jeeraga shala/kaima), cooked to 80%
                                                        • Fresh mint and coriander leaves
                                                        • 1/2 onion, thinly sliced (for crispy topping)
                                                        • 1 tsp oil (for crispy onions)
                                                        • 8–10 roasted cashew nuts

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Marinate the prawns with all marinade ingredients and set aside for 10–15 minutes.
                                                        2. Heat coconut oil + ghee in a wide pan. Add cardamom pods and bay leaf. Once aromatic, discard the whole spices.
                                                        3. Add sliced onions and chopped green chili. Sauté with a pinch of salt till golden brown.
                                                        4. Add tomatoes and cook until soft.
                                                        5. Add marinated prawns and cook for 3–4 minutes. Sprinkle a bit more of the spice mix if desired.
                                                        6. Pour in coconut milk, let it simmer. Adjust salt and add a pinch of sugar if needed.
                                                        7. Stir in chopped coriander, reduce flame, and layer the rice on top.
                                                        8. To make crispy onions: spread thinly sliced onions on a microwave-safe plate, sprinkle with a pinch of salt, brush with oil, and microwave on high in intervals of 3–4 minutes (stirring in between) for 12–14 minutes until crisp and golden.
                                                        9. Garnish the biryani with mint, coriander, crispy onions, and roasted cashews. Cover tightly and cook on the lowest flame for 5–7 minutes. Let rest before serving.

                                                        Steak with Chimichurri, Fries & Caesar Salad

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A full steakhouse-style plate at home: butter-basted NY strip, fresh chimichurri, double-fried fries, and a homemade Caesar salad.

                                                        Steak

                                                        • 2 NY strip steaks
                                                        • Salt & freshly ground black pepper
                                                        • 2 tbsp butter
                                                        • 2 garlic cloves, smashed
                                                        • 1 sprig rosemary

                                                        Chimichurri

                                                        • 3 garlic cloves
                                                        • 1/2 shallot, finely chopped
                                                        • 1 cup fresh parsley, chopped
                                                        • 1 tbsp fresh oregano, chopped
                                                        • 2 tbsp champagne vinegar
                                                        • Salt, to taste
                                                        • 1/4 cup olive oil

                                                        Fries

                                                        • 2 large russet potatoes
                                                        • Neutral oil, for frying
                                                        • Sea salt, to taste

                                                        Caesar Salad

                                                        • 1 head romaine lettuce, chopped
                                                        • 1/2 cup toasted breadcrumbs
                                                        • Freshly grated Parmesan cheese

                                                        Caesar Dressing

                                                        • 1 egg yolk
                                                        • 1 tsp Worcestershire sauce
                                                        • 1 tsp Dijon mustard
                                                        • Juice of 1 lemon
                                                        • 1/3 cup olive oil
                                                        • 2 tbsp grated Parmesan
                                                        • Salt & pepper, to taste

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Steak: Season steaks generously with salt and pepper. Let sit at room temp ~30 minutes.
                                                        2. Steak: Heat a skillet over high heat until hot. Sear steaks 2-3 min per side.
                                                        3. Steak: Add the butter, garlic, and rosemary. Tilt the pan and baste the steaks with the melted butter for 1-2 more minutes, or until desired doneness.
                                                        4. Steak: Let rest 5-10 minutes before slicing.
                                                        5. Chimichurri: In a mortar and pestle (or bowl), mash the garlic with a pinch of salt.
                                                        6. Chimichurri: Add the parsley, shallot, and oregano. Pound to a coarse paste.
                                                        7. Chimichurri: Mix in the vinegar, then slowly stir in the olive oil until emulsified. Adjust salt to taste.
                                                        8. Fries: Slice the potatoes into thin fries. Soak in ice water for 15 minutes.
                                                        9. Fries: Drain and pat dry completely.
                                                        10. Fries: Heat the oil to 325F (160C). Fry the potatoes until just tender (5-6 min). Remove and drain.
                                                        11. Fries: Raise the oil temp to 375F (190C). Flash fry until golden and crispy (2-3 min).
                                                        12. Fries: Drain on paper towels and sprinkle with sea salt.
                                                        13. Caesar salad: In a tall cup, combine the egg yolk, Worcestershire, Dijon, lemon juice, Parmesan, salt & pepper.
                                                        14. Caesar salad: Use an immersion blender to blend while slowly streaming in the olive oil until creamy.
                                                        15. Caesar salad: Toss the romaine with the dressing, breadcrumbs, and extra Parmesan.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Cacio e Pepe Egg Sandwich

                                                        https://justinesnacks.com/cacio-e-pepe-egg-sandwich/

                                                        A breakfast sandwich inspired by cacio e pepe — a runny fried egg with a fluffy pile of freshly grated parmesan and plenty of black pepper on a toasted croissant.

                                                        Sandwich

                                                        • Extra-virgin olive oil
                                                        • 1 large egg
                                                        • Diamond Crystal kosher salt
                                                        • Freshly ground black pepper
                                                        • 1 large croissant (or two slices of your bread of choice)
                                                        • 1 tablespoon fresh rosemary leaves, optional but very good
                                                        • 1/4 large avocado (~40g)
                                                        • 1 ounce parmesan, whole, not grated

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Set a large cast iron skillet over medium heat. Drizzle in two tablespoons of olive oil and let the skillet heat up for 2 minutes, until the oil is hot but not smoking.
                                                        2. Fry the egg in the oil. Add it to the pan for a minute, seasoning with salt and pepper, then flip and finish for another 30 seconds. The yolk will stay runny this way. Remove from the pan.
                                                        3. Use a serrated knife to gently halve the croissant. Toast this in the cast iron pan until golden, about 2 minutes.
                                                        4. Take the croissant out of the pan and fry the rosemary in any remaining oil. This takes about a minute.
                                                        5. To assemble, mash 1/4 of a ripe avocado on one piece of the croissant, season with salt and pepper, then add the egg.
                                                        6. Use a rasp-style grater to grate the parmesan on top, aiming to make it into a nice, fluffy pile. Top with the fried rosemary, and grate way more freshly ground black pepper on top than you think you need — that's the "e pepe" part!
                                                        7. Top with the remaining croissant piece and serve warm.

                                                        Serves 1. If feeling extra cheesy, swap the avocado for ricotta. Don't melt the parmesan in the pan — the fluffy grated pile melts into the yolk in the sandwich instead.

                                                        The Best Chocolate Chip Muffins

                                                        https://butternutbakeryblog.com/best-chocolate-chip-muffins/

                                                        Fluffy chocolate chip muffins with tall domed tops, melty mini chips throughout, and a crunchy sugar crust.

                                                        Dry

                                                        • 2 1/2 cups (320g) all purpose flour
                                                        • 1 cup (200g) granulated sugar
                                                        • 2 1/2 tsp baking powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp baking soda
                                                        • 1/2 tsp salt
                                                        • 1 1/2 cups mini semi sweet chocolate chips, divided

                                                        Wet

                                                        • 2 large eggs, room temp
                                                        • 1/4 cup (65g) sour cream, room temp
                                                        • 2 tsp vanilla extract
                                                        • 2 tbsp pure maple syrup
                                                        • 2 tbsp vegetable oil
                                                        • 1/2 cup (110g) unsalted butter, melted
                                                        • 1 cup (255ml) buttermilk, room temp
                                                        • 2-3 tbsp coarse or raw sugar, for sprinkling

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. In a large mixing bowl, whisk together the flour, sugar, baking powder, baking soda, salt, and 1 cup of the mini chocolate chips.
                                                        2. In a separate bowl, whisk together the eggs and sour cream until completely smooth.
                                                        3. Then mix in the vanilla, maple syrup, and oil, followed by the butter and then the buttermilk.
                                                        4. Pour the wet ingredients into the bowl of dry ingredients and gently whisk to combine. Stop right when the batter is fully combined — careful not to overmix.
                                                        5. Cover the bowl with plastic wrap and let it rest at room temperature for 1 hour. This gives the leaveners a head start so each muffin bakes with a tall muffin top. (If you're in a rush, you can bake the batter right away — they just won't be as domed on top.)
                                                        6. After 1 hour, preheat the oven to 425F and line a muffin (or cupcake) tin with 12 paper liners.
                                                        7. Using an ice cream scoop, gently run the scoop through the bowl of batter. The batter will be very fluffy — be careful not to disrupt it as you scoop; just run the scoop through and pull out the batter.
                                                        8. Overfill each liner just a smidge. You may have about 1 scoop of batter leftover, which is expected.
                                                        9. Top each muffin with a sprinkle of coarse sugar and a pile of mini chocolate chips.
                                                        10. Bake at 425F for 8 minutes. Then, with the oven door still closed, reduce the temperature to 350F and bake for another 8-10 minutes.
                                                        11. Quickly test a muffin with a toothpick to see if they're ready, but don't keep the oven door open too long so the hot air doesn't escape.
                                                        12. Remove the muffins and let them cool for 10 minutes. Then carefully transfer each muffin to a cooling rack (use a knife to pop them loose if they stick at the bottom edges).
                                                        13. Let the muffins cool for another 10-15 minutes. They're best served warm, so enjoy!

                                                        Makes 12. Store cooled muffins in an airtight container at room temp ~2 days, or refrigerate up to 5 days. Full-fat Greek yogurt can stand in for the sour cream.

                                                        View reel

                                                        One Pot Golden Chicken & Rice

                                                        https://britacooks.com/one-pot-golden-chicken-and-rice/

                                                        Crispy-skinned chicken thighs and golden turmeric rice cooked together in one pot, with a bright herby yogurt sauce.

                                                        Chicken & Rice

                                                        • 4 bone-in, skin-on chicken thighs
                                                        • 1 cup rice
                                                        • 2 cups chicken broth
                                                        • 1 small onion, chopped
                                                        • 3 cloves garlic, minced
                                                        • 1 tbsp fresh ginger, minced/grated
                                                        • 1 tsp ground coriander (+ extra for seasoning chicken)
                                                        • 1 tsp turmeric
                                                        • 1/2 tsp crushed red pepper flakes
                                                        • Salt & pepper
                                                        • Splash of coconut milk, optional

                                                        Herby Yogurt Sauce

                                                        • Handful of fresh herbs: cilantro + any mix of parsley, dill, chives, basil, or mint
                                                        • 1 lime, zested & juiced
                                                        • 1/2 cup Greek yogurt
                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Pat the chicken thighs dry, then season with salt, pepper, and a little ground coriander. Rub it in well.
                                                        2. Place the thighs skin-side down in a cold pan. Turn the heat to medium and let the fat slowly render until the skin is golden and crisp, about 6-8 minutes. Flip and cook another 2 minutes on the other side, then set aside.
                                                        3. Spoon off any excess fat, leaving about 2 tablespoons in the pan. Add the onion and cook until softened, a few minutes. Stir in the garlic and ginger and cook 1-2 minutes more.
                                                        4. Sprinkle in the spices (1 tsp turmeric, 1 tsp ground coriander, and a pinch of red pepper flakes). Stir for 30 seconds until fragrant.
                                                        5. Add the rice, stirring to coat, and let it toast for about a minute.
                                                        6. Pour in the stock (you can splash in some coconut milk too if you like), season with a pinch of salt, and scrape up any browned bits from the pan.
                                                        7. Bring to a gentle simmer, then nestle the chicken back in skin-side up, keeping the skin above the liquid. Cover, reduce the heat to low, and cook for 12 minutes. Turn off the heat and let sit, still covered, for 5 minutes.
                                                        8. While the rice cooks, blend together the herbs, lime juice and zest, yogurt, olive oil, and salt until smooth.
                                                        9. Fluff the rice, spoon into bowls, top with the chicken, and drizzle with the green sauce. Serve with extra lime wedges and fresh herbs if you like.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Butter Chicken, Garlic Naan & Spicy Okra

                                                        https://abrightmoment.com/butter-chicken-garlic-naan-spicy-okra/

                                                        A cozy fall-ready trio: creamy butter chicken with a two-step yogurt marinade, homemade garlicky naan, and oven-roasted spicy okra.

                                                        Garlic Naan

                                                        • 1 batch homemade naan dough (linked recipe)
                                                        • 5 garlic cloves, divided
                                                        • 3 tbsp butter, melted
                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1/2 tsp fine sea salt
                                                        • 1/4 cup fresh parsley, chopped

                                                        First Marinade

                                                        • 1 1/2 lbs chicken thighs, cut into bite-sized pieces
                                                        • 1 tsp Kashmiri red chili powder (or regular chili powder)
                                                        • 1 tsp salt
                                                        • Juice of 1 lemon

                                                        Second Marinade

                                                        • 1/2 cup plain yogurt
                                                        • 1 tbsp ginger-garlic paste (or 2 garlic cloves + 1" fresh ginger)
                                                        • 1 tsp EACH: garam masala, ground cumin, dried fenugreek leaves
                                                        • 1/2 tsp ground turmeric

                                                        Butter Chicken Sauce

                                                        • 1 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 yellow onion
                                                        • 1 lb tomatoes, quartered
                                                        • 1/3 cup cashews, soaked in hot water for 10 minutes
                                                        • 3 tbsp butter
                                                        • 2 cinnamon sticks (or 1 tsp ground cinnamon)
                                                        • 4 green cardamom pods
                                                        • 4 whole cloves
                                                        • 1 tbsp honey
                                                        • 1 tsp EACH: ground cumin, Kashmiri red chili powder, garam masala
                                                        • 1/3 cup heavy cream

                                                        Spicy Okra

                                                        • 1-2 lbs okra
                                                        • 3 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 tsp EACH: ground coriander, ground cumin, smoked paprika, cayenne, salt
                                                        • Lemon wedges, for serving

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Combine all first marinade ingredients in a large bowl. Mix well, cover, and marinate in the fridge for 20-30 minutes.
                                                        2. Combine the second marinade ingredients into the chicken bowl and mix until very well combined. Marinate in the fridge 1 hour to overnight — the longer, the more tender and flavorful the chicken.
                                                        3. In a large skillet, add the olive oil, onion, and tomatoes. Sauté 5-6 minutes until fragrant and slightly caramelized. Transfer to a high-speed blender with the drained soaked cashews and pulse until very smooth. Set aside.
                                                        4. In the same skillet, add the butter, cinnamon, green cardamom, and whole cloves. Cook 1-2 minutes until fragrant and the butter is slightly browned. Remove to a small bowl.
                                                        5. Add the chicken to the skillet and cook 4-5 minutes, adding a drizzle of olive oil as needed.
                                                        6. Add the tomato sauce, the butter and spices, honey, remaining ground spices, and heavy cream. Stir until well combined and simmer 10 minutes.
                                                        7. For the garlic naan: make the dough per the linked recipe, adding 3 minced garlic cloves into the dough before the first rise. In a bowl, combine the remaining minced garlic, chopped parsley, melted butter, olive oil, and salt; brush on the cooked naan before serving.
                                                        8. For the spicy okra: preheat the oven to 425F and line a baking pan with parchment. Toss the okra with olive oil and the spices until well combined. Roast 12-15 minutes until tender and slightly charred. Serve warm with lemon wedges.

                                                        Serves 4. Butter chicken tastes even better the next day; store in the fridge 3-4 days or freeze up to 3 months.

                                                        Achamma's (Grandma's) Chilli Beef

                                                        A Kerala-style dry-roasted chilli beef from the creator's grandmother — marinated chuck pressure-cooked until melting, then charred in a fiery shallot masala.

                                                        Beef & marinade

                                                        • 1 kg beef (chuck steak works), cut into bite-sized chunks
                                                        • 2 tsp Kashmiri chilli powder
                                                        • 1 tsp turmeric
                                                        • salt and pepper
                                                        • 1 tbsp coconut oil
                                                        • juice of half a lemon

                                                        For pressure cooking

                                                        • 1 cup water

                                                        Spice mix

                                                        • 10 garlic cloves
                                                        • 1-inch piece ginger
                                                        • 1 tsp peppercorns
                                                        • 1 tsp fennel seeds
                                                        • 1 dried red chilli
                                                        • salt

                                                        For the roast

                                                        • 2 tbsp coconut oil
                                                        • 1 tsp fennel seeds
                                                        • fresh curry leaves
                                                        • 10–20 thinly sliced shallots (or small onions)
                                                        • 2 tsp Kashmiri chilli powder
                                                        • 3 tsp coriander powder
                                                        • 3 tsp meat masala (or more)
                                                        • 1 tsp garam masala
                                                        • 2 diced tomatoes (or canned)
                                                        • extra ground pepper, to finish
                                                        • squeeze of lemon, to finish

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Cut the beef into bite-sized chunks and marinate with the Kashmiri chilli powder, turmeric, salt, pepper, coconut oil, and the squeezed half-lemon. Massage well and rest for 1–2 hours.
                                                        2. Pressure cook the beef with just 1 cup of water — 7–8 whistles or 25 minutes in the Instant Pot — until melt-in-mouth tender. Save 3–4 cups of the stock.
                                                        3. Blend the garlic, ginger, peppercorns, fennel seeds, dried red chilli, and salt into a rough paste (not smooth).
                                                        4. Heat the coconut oil in a big skillet. Sizzle the fennel seeds, add the curry leaves and thinly sliced shallots, and sauté until softened. Add the spice mix and cook until golden.
                                                        5. Stir in the Kashmiri chilli powder, coriander powder, meat masala, and garam masala. Add the diced tomatoes (don't overdo it — this isn't a gravy recipe) and cook, stirring constantly, until the oil separates and the mix darkens. Don't burn it.
                                                        6. Toss in the beef and 1 cup of the reserved stock. Stir occasionally and let the meat roast, char, and soak up all the spices — add a little oil if it sticks.
                                                        7. Finish with extra ground pepper and a squeeze of lemon. Serve with Kerala porottas or any flatbread.

                                                        Easy 5-Minute Sweet & Spicy Udon Lunch

                                                        A quick 5-minute sweet-and-spicy udon noodle lunch. The caption gives the full sauce/ingredient list with brief assembly notes; no detailed cooking steps are provided.

                                                        Sauce & Noodles

                                                        • 1 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 3 cloves of garlic, chopped
                                                        • 1 tbsp honey
                                                        • 1 heaping tbsp brown sugar
                                                        • 1 tbsp soy sauce
                                                        • 1 pack of udon noodles

                                                        Low-Carb Chuck Roast Quesadillas with Cilantro-Lime Sauce

                                                        A slow-roasted chuck roast in curry paste and bone broth that becomes weeknight-friendly high-protein meals, like crispy low-carb quesadillas with a low-calorie cilantro-lime dipping sauce.

                                                        One-Pot Roast

                                                        • 1/2 large yellow onion, sliced
                                                        • 3 lbs chuck roast
                                                        • 4-5 garlic cloves, minced
                                                        • 2 tbsp red curry paste
                                                        • 1.5 tbsp sweet paprika
                                                        • A couple generous pinches of salt and pepper
                                                        • 18-24 oz bone broth

                                                        Cilantro-Lime Sauce

                                                        • 1 large handful cilantro
                                                        • 2 garlic cloves
                                                        • 1/2 jalapeño
                                                        • 3 heaping tbsp plain yogurt
                                                        • Juice of 1 large lime
                                                        • Salt and pepper, to taste

                                                        Crispy Quesadilla (per quesadilla)

                                                        • 1 serving shredded cheese (used in two halves)
                                                        • 1 low-carb tortilla
                                                        • 2 oz of the shredded roast
                                                        • Toppings: jalapeño, cilantro, avocado, onion, or whatever you like

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Grab an oven-safe dish or pot with a lid. Add the sliced half large yellow onion and the 3 lbs chuck roast.
                                                        2. Add the 4-5 minced garlic cloves, 2 tbsp red curry paste, and season with 1.5 tbsp sweet paprika plus a couple generous pinches of salt and pepper.
                                                        3. Pour in 18-24 oz of bone broth. Put the lid on and pop into the oven at 325°F for 3.5 hours. Remove, shred the meat using two forks, then put it back in the oven for 1-2 hours at 400°F. (The creator notes you can reduce the time if you want, but this timing gives the most flavor and buttery texture.)
                                                        4. For the sauce, process the large handful of cilantro in a food processor with 2 garlic cloves, half a jalapeño, 3 heaping tablespoons plain yogurt, the juice of 1 large lime, salt, and pepper. Taste and adjust as you like. Make extra — it goes well on any protein.
                                                        5. To make the crispy quesadilla, bring a pan to medium heat. Add half a serving of shredded cheese; when it starts to bubble, add your favorite low-carb tortilla and 2 oz of the shredded roast. Flip, top with the other half serving of cheese, then fold.
                                                        6. Top with jalapeño, cilantro, avocado, onion, or whatever else you like, and dip in the cilantro sauce. Enjoy!

                                                        Caption numbering jumps from step 3 to step 5 (there is no step 4); transcribed as written. The creator offers a cookbook (100+ recipes) via comment 'SHREDHAPPENS'.

                                                        Air-Fryer Rice Paper Egg Puffs

                                                        Guilt-free, crispy egg puffs made with rice paper and air-fried to perfection — the ultimate healthy twist on a favorite bakery snack, with a spiced onion masala filling.

                                                        Ingredients

                                                        • 2 boiled eggs, cut in halves
                                                        • 4 rice paper sheets
                                                        • 1 medium onion, sliced
                                                        • 1/2 tsp ginger-garlic paste
                                                        • 1/2 tsp garam masala
                                                        • 1/2 tsp Kashmiri chilli powder
                                                        • 1/3 tsp turmeric
                                                        • Salt to taste
                                                        • 1 tbsp oil
                                                        • Curry leaves (optional)
                                                        • Water for softening rice paper
                                                        • Ghee or oil for brushing

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Heat the oil in a pan. Sauté the onions with a pinch of salt until soft and golden.
                                                        2. Add the ginger-garlic paste and sauté till the raw smell goes.
                                                        3. Add the turmeric, chilli powder, and garam masala, and mix well.
                                                        4. Add curry leaves (if using) and sauté for 1 more minute.
                                                        5. Turn off the heat and let the mixture cool.
                                                        6. Dip one rice paper sheet in water for 10-15 seconds until just soft, and place it on a clean surface.
                                                        7. Add a spoon of the onion masala in the center. Place half a boiled egg (cut side down) over the masala.
                                                        8. Fold the rice paper over like a parcel (square or roll shape). If the paper tears or feels thin, wrap it in another softened sheet.
                                                        9. Repeat for the remaining eggs.
                                                        10. Preheat the air fryer to 180°C (350°F). Lightly brush each puff with ghee or oil.
                                                        11. Place in the air fryer basket and cook at 180°C for 8-10 minutes or until golden and crisp. Flip halfway and brush the other side if needed for even crispiness.

                                                        Chicken Shawarma Crispy Rice Salad

                                                        The creator's most viral recipe: chicken shawarma over crispy oven-baked rice, tossed with a crunchy cucumber-tomato salad and a creamy lemon-tahini dressing.

                                                        Crispy Rice

                                                        • 2 cups cooked rice, cooled (jasmine)
                                                        • 1 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 tsp paprika, 1 tsp oregano, 1/2 tsp garlic powder

                                                        Chicken

                                                        • 1 lb boneless, skinless chicken thighs, cut into 1/2-inch pieces
                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 2 tsp ground turmeric
                                                        • 2 tsp cumin
                                                        • 1 tsp cinnamon
                                                        • 1 tsp garlic powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp black pepper

                                                        Salad

                                                        • 5 Persian cucumbers, thinly sliced
                                                        • 1 cup baby tomatoes, halved
                                                        • 1/2 red onion, finely sliced (1/2 cup)
                                                        • 3 dill pickles, chopped
                                                        • 1 bunch mint, chopped (1/2 cup)

                                                        Creamy Lemon-Tahini Dressing

                                                        • 1/2 cup Greek yogurt
                                                        • 1/3 cup tahini
                                                        • 1/4 cup olive oil
                                                        • 1/4 cup lemon juice (1 lemon)
                                                        • 2 cloves garlic
                                                        • 1 tsp kosher salt
                                                        • optional: 1–2 tbsp honey or maple syrup for sweetness

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 400°F. Spread the cooked, cooled rice on a baking sheet, toss with the olive oil and seasonings, and bake for 30–35 minutes, tossing halfway through (watch so it doesn't burn).
                                                        2. While the rice bakes, season the chicken pieces with the spices and coat well. Heat the olive oil in a skillet over medium heat, add the chicken, and cook until fully cooked through; remove from heat.
                                                        3. Remove the rice from the oven and set aside.
                                                        4. Chop/slice all the salad ingredients and add to a large bowl. Add the chicken and crispy rice on top.
                                                        5. Make the dressing: blend all dressing ingredients until smooth and creamy. Taste and adjust seasonings as needed.
                                                        6. Pour the dressing over the salad, toss, and enjoy!

                                                        Makes 4 large servings.

                                                        High Protein Chicken Caesar Salad Pasta

                                                        A high-protein, low-carb chicken caesar pasta salad with a Greek yogurt–anchovy caesar-style dressing. Made with Kaizen high-protein pasta (20g protein, 6g net carbs per serving), though any protein or low-carb pasta works.

                                                        Chicken

                                                        • 1 lb chicken thighs
                                                        • 1 tbsp avocado oil
                                                        • Juice of 1 lemon
                                                        • 1 tbsp smoked paprika
                                                        • 1 tsp oregano
                                                        • 1 tsp onion powder
                                                        • 1/2 tsp chili flakes

                                                        Caesar Dressing

                                                        • 3 tbsp avocado oil mayo
                                                        • 3 tbsp thick plain Greek yogurt
                                                        • 1 tbsp Dijon mustard
                                                        • 1.5 tbsp apple cider vinegar
                                                        • Juice of 1 lemon
                                                        • 1 garlic clove, minced
                                                        • 3 anchovy filets (optional but 🔥)
                                                        • Salt, to taste
                                                        • Lots of black pepper
                                                        • 1/4 cup shredded Parmesan
                                                        • Water or lemon juice, to thin out as needed

                                                        Salad

                                                        • 2 heads of romaine, finely chopped
                                                        • 6-8 oz Kaizen low-carb/high-protein pasta (or any high-protein pasta)
                                                        • Extra shredded Parmesan, for topping

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Start with the chicken: mix 1 lb chicken thighs with 1 tbsp avocado oil, juice of 1 lemon, 1 tbsp smoked paprika, 1 tsp oregano, 1 tsp onion powder, and 1/2 tsp chili flakes. Mix well.
                                                        2. Roast the chicken at 425°F for 34-36 minutes, then dice it up.
                                                        3. Make the dressing: mix 3 tbsp avocado oil mayo, 3 tbsp thick plain Greek yogurt, 1 tbsp Dijon, 1.5 tbsp apple cider vinegar, juice of 1 lemon, 1 minced garlic clove, 3 anchovy filets (optional), salt, lots of pepper, and 1/4 cup shredded Parm. Thin it out with water or lemon juice if needed. Taste and adjust.
                                                        4. Finely chop 2 heads of romaine.
                                                        5. Cook 6-8 oz of Kaizen low-carb pasta (or any high-protein pasta).
                                                        6. Assemble: layer cubed chicken with the romaine and cooked pasta in a big jar. Flip it onto a plate for that chef's kiss pasta presentation.
                                                        7. Drizzle with dressing and extra Parm on top. Enjoy!

                                                        Chocolate Truffle Cake

                                                        https://scientificallysweet.com/chocolate-truffle-cake/

                                                        Three layers of pure chocolate decadence: moist chocolate cake, mousse-like whipped chocolate truffle, and silky dark chocolate ganache, topped with chocolate curls. From Scientifically Sweet.

                                                        Chocolate Cake Batter

                                                        • 1 cup (142g) all-purpose flour
                                                        • 1/3 cup (28g) cocoa powder (Dutch process preferred)
                                                        • 1/2 teaspoon baking soda
                                                        • 1/4 teaspoon baking powder
                                                        • 3/4 cup plus 2 tbsp (175g) granulated sugar
                                                        • 1/4 teaspoon salt
                                                        • 1/3 cup (80ml) sunflower oil
                                                        • 2 large eggs, well beaten
                                                        • 1/2 teaspoon pure vanilla extract
                                                        • 1/3 cup (80ml) full fat sour cream
                                                        • 1/2 cup (120ml) hot water (just off the boil)

                                                        Whipped Chocolate Truffle

                                                        • 7 oz (200g) dark chocolate (55-60% cocoa solids), coarsely chopped
                                                        • 1 1/2 cups (355ml) 35% heavy whipping cream, divided
                                                        • 1 tablespoon (15ml) corn syrup or honey
                                                        • 1 tablespoon (6g) cocoa powder

                                                        Chocolate Glaze

                                                        • 4 oz (113g) semisweet dark chocolate, finely chopped
                                                        • 1/3 cup plus 1 tbsp (95ml) 35% heavy whipping cream
                                                        • 1 teaspoon (5ml) light corn syrup or honey

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat oven to 350F. Line the base of a 9-inch round springform pan with parchment and lightly grease the sides.
                                                        2. Make the cake: sift flour, cocoa powder, baking powder and baking soda into a bowl; add sugar and salt and whisk. Add oil, beaten eggs, sour cream and vanilla and whisk gently (it will be thick), then add the hot water and whisk until smooth.
                                                        3. Pour into the pan and bake 20-25 minutes until a skewer comes out clean. Cool completely in the pan.
                                                        4. Remove the cake from the pan, peel off the parchment, and trim the domed top level with a serrated knife. Place the cake back into the springform pan.
                                                        5. Make the truffle layer: heat 1/2 cup of the cream with the honey until simmering and pour over the chopped chocolate. Cover, stand 2 minutes, then whisk gently until smooth and glossy. Let cool until no longer warm but not set.
                                                        6. Whip the remaining 1 cup of cream with the cocoa powder to soft peaks. Fold one-third into the cooled ganache, then fold in the rest gently until no streaks remain.
                                                        7. Spoon the truffle filling over the leveled cake, smooth the top, and chill 2 hours to set.
                                                        8. Make the glaze: heat the cream and honey until just simmering, pour over the chopped chocolate and whisk gently until smooth. Cool about 15 minutes until slightly warm but still fluid, pour over the chilled cake and smooth quickly. Chill until set.
                                                        9. Serve chilled or at room temperature (leave out 2 hours before serving to soften). Store refrigerated.

                                                        Do not whip or beat the ganache - stir gently so the fat doesn't separate. Whip the cream only to soft peaks for a delicate truffle texture. The cake stays moist straight from the fridge.

                                                        View reel

                                                        Fish Moilee (Kerala Style Fish Stew)

                                                        An easy one-pot Kerala-style fish stew in coconut milk - marinated fish simmered with onion, capsicum, curry leaves and gentle spices.

                                                        For Marination

                                                        • King fish - 8 medium thick slices (or prawns, pomfret, seabream, trevally)
                                                        • Pepper powder - 2 tsp
                                                        • Ginger garlic paste - 1/2 tbsp
                                                        • Turmeric - 1 tsp
                                                        • Salt

                                                        For Gravy

                                                        • Onion - 1 big, cut in medium cubes
                                                        • Capsicum - 1, cubed
                                                        • Garlic - 1 tbsp crushed
                                                        • Ginger - 1 tbsp crushed
                                                        • Pepper - 2 tsp (more or less)
                                                        • Green chillies - 4 (more or less)
                                                        • Curry leaves - 2 tbsp
                                                        • Tomatoes - 1 small, cut in rounds
                                                        • Cinnamon - 1 stick
                                                        • Water - 1 cup
                                                        • Coconut milk - 1 cup
                                                        • Garam masala - 1 tsp (optional)
                                                        • Coconut oil
                                                        • Salt

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Marinate the fish with pepper powder, ginger garlic paste, turmeric and salt for 15-20 minutes.
                                                        2. Heat coconut oil in a pan and lightly fry the fish (no need to deep fry or crisp it); set aside.
                                                        3. In the same pan, saute the garlic and capsicum; set aside.
                                                        4. In the same pan add the cinnamon stick, onions, green chillies, curry leaves and ginger; saute well. Add salt, pepper, garam masala and water.
                                                        5. When the water warms, add the fried fish, tomatoes and the sauteed capsicum and garlic; cover with a lid and cook for some time.
                                                        6. Pour in the thick coconut milk and warm through once - do not boil the gravy after adding coconut milk. Ready!

                                                        Any firm fish works. The coconut milk should only be warmed, never boiled, to keep the gravy silky.
