                                                        High Protein Chicken Pad Thai

                                                        A high-protein pad thai with chicken, rice noodles, bok choy and a tamarind sauce - 493 cal and 52g protein per serving, makes 4 servings.

                                                        Pad Thai

                                                        • 21oz chicken thigh (or breast)
                                                        • 1/2 onion, sliced
                                                        • 1 head garlic, minced
                                                        • 2 cups bok choy, bean sprouts or veggies of choice
                                                        • 1 cup green onion
                                                        • 1/4 cup crushed peanuts
                                                        • 2 eggs + 1/2 cup egg whites (or 4 eggs)
                                                        • Half a pack of rice noodles

                                                        Sauce

                                                        • 1/2 cup tamarind paste
                                                        • 3 tbsp oyster sauce
                                                        • 1 tbsp dark soy sauce
                                                        • 3 tbsp fish sauce
                                                        • 4 tbsp palm sugar or brown sugar

                                                        Steps

                                                        2. Prep and cut all the veggies and chicken.

                                                        4. Add the bok choy or veggie of choice, then the rice noodles and green onions.

                                                        Pistachio Butter

                                                        Homemade pistachio butter with white chocolate - blend roasted pistachios with oil until buttery; great on bread or ice cream. Keeps about 10 days refrigerated.

                                                        Pistachio Butter

                                                        • Pistachios - 100g
                                                        • White chocolate - 50g
                                                        • Oil - 4 tbsp

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Put the pistachios in hot water for 10 minutes.
                                                        2. Remove the skins and let them pan dry.
                                                        3. Roast in the oven or air fryer on low temperature. Let them cool down.
                                                        4. Put the pistachios, white chocolate and oil in the mixer jar and blend until it turns into a buttery texture.
                                                        5. Store for around 10 days in the fridge; enjoy with any breads or ice creams.

                                                        While grinding, don't add water at all - use only oil if needed to make it smooth. The residual heat in the pistachios helps release moisture and make it buttery. You can use sugar-free milk chocolate or milk powder with sugar instead of white chocolate.

                                                        Prawn Karahi

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A quick tomato-garlic prawn karahi finished with cream — ready in minutes, served hot with naan.

                                                        Prawn Marinade

                                                        • 680 g large prawns
                                                        • 2 tsp paprika
                                                        • 1/4 tsp salt
                                                        • 1 tsp chilli flakes
                                                        • 1/4 tsp ground black pepper
                                                        • 2 tbsp lemon juice

                                                        Karahi Sauce

                                                        • A little oil
                                                        • 1 whole bulb of garlic, finely chopped
                                                        • 3 green chillies, roughly chopped
                                                        • 2 tomatoes, finely chopped
                                                        • 1 tsp paprika
                                                        • 1/2 tsp chaat masala
                                                        • 1/4 tsp coriander powder
                                                        • 2 tbsp cream
                                                        • Chopped parsley, to garnish
                                                        • Naan, to serve

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Place the prawns in a bowl; add the paprika, salt, chilli flakes, black pepper and lemon juice. Mix well and set aside to marinate while preparing the sauce.
                                                        2. In a karahi, warm a little oil and add the finely chopped garlic. Cook gently until lightly golden.
                                                        3. Add the green chillies and let them sizzle with the garlic for 3 minutes.
                                                        4. Add the finely chopped tomatoes; raise the heat to medium-high and cook 10-12 minutes, stirring often, until the water evaporates and a thick sauce forms.
                                                        5. Add the paprika, chaat masala and coriander powder; cook the masala 6-7 minutes so the spices deepen in flavour.
                                                        6. Add the marinated prawns; lower the heat and stir gently (don't break them). Cook 5-6 minutes only, until the prawns turn just opaque — do not overcook.
                                                        7. Turn off the heat and fold in the cream; return to a low flame for 2 minutes to warm through.
                                                        8. Garnish with chopped parsley and serve hot with naan.
                                                        View reel

                                                        Best Potato Puree

                                                        Silky French-style potato puree made by simmering the potatoes in milk and passing them through a tamis strainer, finished with cold butter and creme fraiche.

                                                        Potato Puree

                                                        • 2 lb yukon gold potatoes (peeled and cubed)
                                                        • 2 1/2 cups whole milk
                                                        • 8-10 oz unsalted butter (cubed)
                                                        • 1 garlic clove
                                                        • 2 tbsp creme fraiche
                                                        • Chives
                                                        • Black pepper
                                                        • Salt
                                                        • Freshly grated nutmeg to taste

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Add the potatoes to a pot with the milk, a pinch of salt and the garlic clove.
                                                        2. Bring to a simmer and cook covered until fork tender.
                                                        3. Remove the garlic. Lift the potatoes out with a slotted spoon and remove 1/3 of the milk from the pot.
                                                        4. Pass the potatoes through a tamis strainer directly into the pot with the remaining milk.
                                                        5. Keeping the heat on low, add the butter a little at a time until fully incorporated. Add nutmeg.
                                                        6. Finish with creme fraiche, adding more of the reserved milk if needed. Turn the heat off and salt to taste.
                                                        7. Top with chives, black pepper and flaky sea salt.

                                                        Simmering the potatoes in milk is the true key - they release natural starches into the milk so you won't need cream. Add cold butter in small bits for maximum creaminess; reheat bain marie.

                                                        Lemongrass Chicken and Coconut Rice

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        High-protein 30-minute dinner: lemongrass-marinated chicken with coconut rice and quick stir-fried greens. Marinating the chicken a day ahead gives extra flavour.

                                                        Chicken Marinade

                                                        • 1 kg skinless chicken thigh fillets
                                                        • 4 lemongrass stalks
                                                        • 1 bunch Thai basil (or coriander/cilantro)
                                                        • 4 long red chillies
                                                        • 1 inch knob ginger
                                                        • 4 spring onion stalks
                                                        • 2 tbsp fish sauce
                                                        • 1 lemon, juiced
                                                        • 1 tbsp sesame oil
                                                        • 1 tsp msg
                                                        • 1 tsp salt and pepper

                                                        Coconut Rice

                                                        • 3 cups jasmine rice, rinsed and drained
                                                        • 1 can coconut milk, plus 1.5 cans of boiling water (3 cups liquid total)
                                                        • 2 bay leaves or kaffir lime leaves
                                                        • Pinch of salt

                                                        Greens

                                                        • 1 bunch Chinese broccoli (or tenderstem/broccolini)
                                                        • 1 bunch sugar snap peas
                                                        • 2 chillies, chopped
                                                        • 1 inch knob ginger, sliced
                                                        • 1/2 lemon, juiced
                                                        • 1 tbsp soy sauce
                                                        • 1 tbsp sesame oil

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Bash the lemongrass with a hard blunt object (back of a knife) until tender, then slice finely.
                                                        2. Add to a food processor with all the other marinade ingredients and blitz until in tiny pieces.
                                                        3. Marinate the chicken for up to a day for maximum flavour, or use straight away.
                                                        4. Air fry at 180C for 20 minutes.
                                                        5. For the rice: rinse well with cold water, then add hot water (equal cups liquid to rice) with a lid and tea towel on top; bring to a boil on high heat, then low heat for 10 min, then off the heat with the lid still on for 10 min.
                                                        6. Quick stir-fry all the veg for 1 min in oil, then 1 min with some of the marinade. Serve over the coconut rice with the chicken.

                                                        Makes 4-5 serves. Done in 30 minutes, healthy and high protein.

                                                        Crispy Potato Balls

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Crispy, crushable potato balls with hashbrown energy — serve with kewpie furikake mayo or any dipping sauce.

                                                        Potato Balls

                                                        • 3 medium gold potatoes
                                                        • 6 tbsp cornstarch
                                                        • 2 tbsp parsley
                                                        • 1 tsp salt
                                                        • 1/2 tsp pepper
                                                        • Neutral cooking oil
                                                        • 3 tbsp parmesan or gruyere cheese, shredded, plus extra for topping
                                                        • Dipping sauce of choice (ranch, furikake kewpie, creamy garlic, etc.)

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Peel and boil the potatoes for 15 min or until tender when poked with a fork.
                                                        2. Mash the potatoes until smooth and let cool to the touch.
                                                        3. Mix in the cornstarch, parsley, shredded cheese, salt and pepper, then form into mini ball shapes.
                                                        4. Fry at 330F until golden brown.

                                                        Frying tips: moisture creates vigorous splashes — season lightly with salt to reduce moisture so the cornstarch can absorb it; freeze the balls 15-30 min to hold their shape; test-fry one ball first to check oil temp and binding; lower balls in with a ladle; keep the temperature at 330F.

                                                        View reel

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        Also called Gooey Butter Cake or Chess Bars — buttery, flaky and perfectly chewy, starting from a box of yellow cake mix.

                                                        Cake Base

                                                        • 2 eggs
                                                        • 1/2 cup butter, melted
                                                        • 1 tsp vanilla extract

                                                        Gooey Topping

                                                        • 3 1/2 cups confectioners' sugar

                                                        • 2 eggs

                                                        Steps

                                                        Pistachio Tiramisu

                                                        https://youthsweets.com/pistachio-tiramisu/

                                                        No-bake, egg-free tiramisu: pistachio coffee-soaked ladyfingers nestled in a whipped pistachio mascarpone cream. The creator swears by pistachio flavoring in the coffee soak.

                                                        Tiramisu

                                                        • 2/3 cup pistachio paste (173 g / 6 oz), store bought or homemade
                                                        • 1 cup mascarpone (8 oz), softened
                                                        • 1 1/4 cups heavy whipping cream (290 g), chilled
                                                        • 1/3 cup granulated sugar (73 g)
                                                        • 1 tsp vanilla extract
                                                        • 1/2 tsp almond extract
                                                        • 1/4 tsp kosher salt
                                                        • 1 tbsp amaretto or other liquor (optional)
                                                        • 2 cups strong-brew coffee
                                                        • 1/4 tsp pistachio flavoring
                                                        • 40-50 ladyfingers (two 3.5 oz boxes)

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Beat the pistachio paste and 1/4 cup of the heavy cream with a hand mixer or whisk until smooth in a large bowl. Add the mascarpone, vanilla, almond extract and salt; beat until completely smooth.
                                                        2. In a separate bowl, combine the remaining 1 cup of heavy cream with the granulated sugar and beat to stiff peaks.
                                                        3. Fold the whipped cream into the mascarpone mixture with a rubber spatula until combined; set aside.
                                                        4. Whisk together the coffee, alcohol (if using) and pistachio flavoring in a shallow dish.
                                                        5. Working with half the ladyfingers, quickly dip each one into the coffee mixture and lay in a single layer over the bottom of a 9x9-inch square pan. Top with half the cream mixture, smoothing to the edges.
                                                        6. Dip the rest of the ladyfingers and make a second layer; top with the remaining cream and smooth the top.
                                                        7. Cover and refrigerate at least 2 hours (resting time) before serving.

                                                        Serves 10. Prep 5 min, cook 10 min, resting 2 hours.

                                                        Copycat McDonald's Chicken Nuggets & Cheeseburgers

                                                        https://abrightmoment.com/copycat-mcdonalds-chicken-nuggets/

                                                        A healthier at-home take on the McDonald's combo: crispy chicken nuggets plus the creator's cheeseburger assembly formula.

                                                        Chicken Nuggets

                                                        • 1 1/2 lbs ground chicken
                                                        • 2 large eggs
                                                        • 2 tsp kosher salt
                                                        • 1 cup cornstarch
                                                        • 1 tbsp onion powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp garlic powder
                                                        • 1 tbsp paprika
                                                        • 1 tsp black pepper
                                                        • 1 cup flour
                                                        • 1 cup club soda
                                                        • Frying oil

                                                        Cheeseburger Formula

                                                        • Ketchup on bottom bun
                                                        • Crisp lettuce
                                                        • Two smash burgers with American cheese
                                                        • Thinly sliced red onion
                                                        • Pickles
                                                        • Mustard on top bun

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. In a food processor, blend the chicken, 1 egg, salt and the seasonings (garlic powder, onion powder, paprika) until paste-like.
                                                        2. Form the chicken into tablespoon-sized nuggets.
                                                        3. Coat the nuggets in cornstarch, then transfer to a parchment-lined baking pan. Freeze 20 minutes.
                                                        4. In a mixing bowl, combine the flour, remaining egg and club soda; season with more garlic powder, onion powder and paprika. Mix to a smooth batter.
                                                        5. In a saucepan over medium-high heat, bring 3-4 inches of frying oil to 350F.
                                                        6. Working in small batches, dip nuggets in the batter and fry 2-3 minutes until floating. Remove with a slotted spoon to a paper-towel-lined plate.
                                                        7. Fry a second time for another 2-3 minutes until golden.
                                                        8. Cool 5-10 minutes before enjoying.

                                                        Serves 4. Prep 20 min, cook 15 min, resting 20 min. The fries from the reel's combo are not detailed on the recipe page.

                                                        Crispy Rice Tuna Salad with Creamy Cilantro Dressing

                                                        https://abrightmoment.com/crispy-rice-tuna-salad-with-creamy-cilantro-dressing/

                                                        High-protein dinner: oven-crisped jasmine rice tossed with tuna, avocado, edamame and crunchy veg in a creamy cilantro-lime dressing.

                                                        Crispy Rice Tuna Salad

                                                        • 2-3 cans Wild Planet Foods wild albacore tuna, drained
                                                        • 1 cup uncooked jasmine rice
                                                        • 1 cup water
                                                        • 1 tsp salt
                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 2 tbsp soy sauce
                                                        • 1 tbsp chili crisp oil
                                                        • 1 English cucumber, chopped
                                                        • 1 jalapeno, seeds removed, finely chopped
                                                        • 1 red onion, finely chopped
                                                        • 3 scallions, chopped
                                                        • 1 cup fresh herbs, chopped (mint, cilantro, dill and/or parsley)
                                                        • 1 ripe avocado, chopped
                                                        • 1 cup edamame beans

                                                        Creamy Cilantro Dressing

                                                        • 1/2 cup plain Greek yogurt
                                                        • 1/3 cup fresh cilantro
                                                        • Juice of 2 limes
                                                        • 2 tbsp rice vinegar
                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 1 tbsp maple syrup or honey

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat oven to 425F. Line a large rimmed baking pan with parchment paper.
                                                        2. Add 1 tbsp olive oil to a large saucepan over medium-high heat. Add the uncooked rice and salt; saute 1-2 minutes. Add the water, bring to a boil, then reduce to a simmer and cook covered 15 minutes.
                                                        3. Spoon the cooked rice onto the prepared pan. Toss with the remaining olive oil, chili crisp oil and soy sauce; spread evenly with the back of a spoon. Bake 30-40 minutes, tossing every 15 minutes, until crispy.
                                                        4. In a food processor, combine all the dressing ingredients and blend until smooth; season with salt and black pepper to taste.
                                                        5. In a large mixing bowl, combine the chopped veggies, crispy rice and dressing; toss well and serve.

                                                        Serves 4. Prep 20 min, cook 40 min.

                                                        Leek Fondue with Seared Scallops

                                                        https://lechefswife.com/leek-fondue-with-seared-scallops/

                                                        A classic French recipe in under 25 minutes: golden seared scallops on a bed of silky leek fondue with buttery potatoes. From Le Chef's Wife (written for Le Creuset's 100-year anniversary).

                                                        Ingredients

                                                        • 8 fresh jumbo sea scallops (about 1.5 inches in diameter)
                                                        • 4 leeks
                                                        • 4 golden potatoes, peeled and quartered
                                                        • 1 shallot, finely chopped
                                                        • 1/2 cup heavy cream
                                                        • 5 tbsp butter
                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil
                                                        • 2 pinches of salt (Sel de Camargue or rock salt)
                                                        • 2 tbsp dry white vermouth (dry white wine also works)
                                                        • 1 lemon, for zesting
                                                        • Parsley, for garnish

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Cut the bottoms and tough green tops off the leeks (use only the light green and white parts); peel off the outer layers, rinse, and cut into thin half-moon slivers.
                                                        2. Put the peeled, quartered potatoes in a saucepan covered with cold water and a pinch of salt; bring to a boil, then simmer 15 minutes until tender. Drain.
                                                        3. In a braiser, heat 1 tbsp olive oil and 2 tbsp butter over medium heat. Add the shallot and a pinch of salt; stir frequently until translucent.
                                                        4. Add the chopped leeks; stir frequently over medium heat so they don't burn, until soft and melty. Transfer to a bowl and set aside; wipe the pan clean.
                                                        5. Rinse the scallops and dry thoroughly with a paper towel (a wet scallop will not sear).
                                                        6. Add 1 tbsp olive oil and 2 tbsp butter to the pan over medium heat. Sear the scallops 2 minutes per side without moving them; remove and set aside.
                                                        7. Deglaze the pan with the white vermouth and the remaining 1 tbsp butter; once simmering, add the leeks back in, scraping up the caramelized bits.
                                                        8. Pour in the heavy cream and stir over medium-low heat.
                                                        9. Nestle the scallops into the leek fondue along with the potatoes; cover and steam 4 minutes on low heat (don't let the leeks brown).
                                                        10. Turn off the heat, uncover, grate lemon zest over the top and sprinkle with parsley. Bon appetit!

                                                        Serves 4. Prep 15 min, cook 20 min. Gluten-free. Tips: dry scallops well for a good sear; don't overcook — they should look pearly white inside; chop leeks very finely so they melt.

                                                        View reel

                                                        Onion Chive Biscuits

                                                        https://themodernproper.com/onion-chive-biscuits

                                                        Fluffy buttermilk biscuits loaded with chives and scallions, with Parmesan in the dough — the perfect savory addition to the table, from The Modern Proper.

                                                        Biscuits

                                                        • 2 1/2 cups all-purpose flour, plus more for dusting
                                                        • 1 tablespoon baking powder
                                                        • 1/2 teaspoon baking soda
                                                        • 1 1/2 teaspoons sea salt
                                                        • 2 tablespoons dried minced onion (optional)
                                                        • 10 tablespoons cold unsalted butter, sliced
                                                        • 1/2 cup freshly grated Parmesan cheese
                                                        • 1 cup cold buttermilk, plus more for brushing
                                                        • 1/2 cup chopped chives
                                                        • 1/2 cup chopped green onions (scallions)

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Preheat the oven to 450F with a rack in the center position. Line a rimmed sheet pan with parchment paper.
                                                        2. To a food processor, add the flour, baking powder, baking soda, salt and minced onion (if using); pulse 2-3 times to combine. Add the butter and Parmesan and pulse until pea-sized crumbles form. Add the buttermilk and pulse 8 times, or until a shaggy dough forms.
                                                        3. Turn the dough out onto a lightly floured surface. Mix the chives and green onions in by hand until just combined and the dough is a little less sticky.
                                                        4. Press the dough into a 1-inch-thick rectangle; fold into thirds and press into a 1-inch-thick rectangle again. Repeat the folding and pressing 3 more times.
                                                        5. Cut out 8 rounds with a 2 1/2-inch biscuit cutter, or cut 9 squares with a sharp knife. Gather scraps, pat into a 1-inch-thick round and cut the rest (9 total).
                                                        6. Arrange on the prepared pan and brush with buttermilk. Bake 18-20 minutes, until golden brown. Cool slightly before serving.

                                                        Makes 9 biscuits. No food processor: pinch the butter in by hand or with a pastry cutter, keeping it cold; don't overwork the dough. Store airtight: up to a week in the fridge, up to 3 months in the freezer.

                                                        Creamy Mushroom and Spinach Orzo

                                                        https://hungryhappens.net/creamy-mushroom-and-spinach-orzo/

                                                        A one-pan creamy orzo with browned mushrooms and spinach that tastes like a gourmet risotto. Serves 4 (prep 5 min, cook 35 min).

                                                        Orzo

                                                        • 2 tbsp olive oil, divided
                                                        • 1 lb baby bella mushrooms, sliced
                                                        • salt and pepper, to taste
                                                        • 2 tbsp salted butter, divided
                                                        • 1 medium onion, fine dice
                                                        • 3 cloves garlic, minced
                                                        • 2 cups uncooked orzo pasta
                                                        • 3 3/4 cups low-sodium chicken broth, room temperature
                                                        • 1/2 cup heavy cream, room temperature
                                                        • 1/2 cup milk, room temperature
                                                        • 1 tsp Italian herb seasoning
                                                        • salt + pepper, to taste
                                                        • 1/2 cup freshly grated parmesan cheese
                                                        • 4 oz baby spinach, rough chop
                                                        • 1/4 cup fresh parsley, chopped

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Add 1 tbsp olive oil to a large deep skillet. Saute the mushrooms in two batches (each with 1 tbsp olive oil), about 5 minutes each batch. Season with salt and pepper to taste. Transfer browned mushrooms to a plate and set aside.
                                                        2. Add 1 tbsp of butter to the pan and the onion. Saute for 2-3 minutes, then stir in the garlic for 30 seconds. Stir in the orzo for 1 minute. Add the mushrooms back to the pan along with the broth, heavy cream, milk, herb blend seasoning and salt and pepper to taste. Bring to a boil, then lower the heat to a simmer. Stir constantly until the orzo is cooked through al dente.
                                                        3. Remove from heat and stir in the remaining 1 tbsp of butter and the grated parmesan. Fold in the spinach and parsley. Serve with extra parmesan or parsley.

                                                        From the creator's website: the broth and cream must be at room temperature when added, or the cream will curdle.

                                                        Spicy Spinach Mushroom Pasta

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A creamy one-pot pasta with browned mushrooms, garlicky spinach and mozzarella on top.

                                                        Pasta and mushrooms

                                                        • 10 oz pasta
                                                        • 20 oz chopped mushrooms (creator used white and oyster mushrooms)
                                                        • 3 garlic, minced
                                                        • 1/2 jalapeno, chopped
                                                        • 1/2 teaspoon salt, black pepper, paprika or cayenne, turmeric

                                                        Spinach

                                                        • 1 tablespoon olive oil
                                                        • 1/2 onion, diced
                                                        • 4 garlic, minced
                                                        • 2 handfuls spinach

                                                        Sauce

                                                        • 2 tablespoons butter
                                                        • 1 tablespoon all-purpose flour
                                                        • 8 oz milk (creator used whole milk)
                                                        • 8 oz broth
                                                        • 2 cups (16 oz) heavy whipping cream
                                                        • 1/4 cup parmesan cheese (caption reads '1/4 Parmesan cheese')
                                                        • 1-2 cups mozzarella cheese

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Cook pasta in salt water - al dente, don't overcook.
                                                        2. Cook mushrooms in butter; after 5-6 minutes, when the mushrooms are cooked and browning, add the garlic, jalapeno and seasoning, then remove.
                                                        3. Saute the onion, garlic and spinach in olive oil. Cook 1-2 min, then remove.
                                                        4. Add the butter and flour on low-medium heat; whisk the flour until brown, 1-2 minutes. Slowly add the milk and broth. Sprinkle in the parmesan. Add the spinach and heavy whipping cream. Cook until it starts to thicken.
                                                        5. Adjust salt and pepper.
                                                        6. Add the pasta, top with the mushrooms and lots of mozzarella.
                                                        7. Cover with the lid. Optional: drizzle chili oil and chili flakes.

                                                        Milk Chocolate Honeycomb Bon Bons

                                                        https://www.donnahay.com.au/recipes/desserts-and-baking/milk-chocolate-honeycomb-bon-bons

                                                        Crisp shards of homemade honeycomb drizzled with milk chocolate - a festive, giftable treat. Serves 10-12.

                                                        Honeycomb and chocolate

                                                        • 1/4 cup (60ml) water
                                                        • 300g caster (superfine) sugar
                                                        • 2 tablespoons honey
                                                        • 180g glucose syrup
                                                        • 1 tablespoon bicarbonate of soda, sifted
                                                        • 270g milk chocolate, melted

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Line a 20cm x 30cm deep baking tray with non-stick baking paper.
                                                        2. Combine the water, sugar, honey and glucose in a large heavy-based saucepan. Place over low heat and stir, without boiling, until all the sugar has dissolved.
                                                        3. Increase the heat to medium and bring to a boil. Add a sugar (candy) thermometer and cook until the temperature reaches 148C (298F).
                                                        4. Remove from the heat and, working quickly, whisk in the bicarbonate of soda until well combined. Immediately pour the mixture into the prepared tray. Allow to cool and harden in a cool dry place. Do not refrigerate.
                                                        5. Cut into large shards and place on non-stick baking paper. Drizzle the tops of the honeycomb with the chocolate and allow to cool.
                                                        6. Store in an airtight container. To give as a gift, wrap in sheets of non-stick baking paper and tie the ends with string or ribbon.

                                                        From the creator's website (the reel caption itself only points to the website). The creator's cook's tip: once the sugar has dissolved, do not stir while the temperature rises, and keep the thermometer off the base of the pan.

                                                        Amma's Style Easy Peasy Chicken Dum Biriyani

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A home-style one-pot chicken dum biryani with a fresh coriander-chilli paste and whole spices.

                                                        Paste

                                                        • 1 cup coriander leaves
                                                        • 6-7 green chillies

                                                        Masala

                                                        • oil, for cooking
                                                        • 1 bay leaf
                                                        • 2 cloves
                                                        • 2 small cinnamon sticks
                                                        • 2 cardamom
                                                        • 1 star anise
                                                        • 2 marati moggu (kapok buds)
                                                        • 1 small japatre (nutmeg mace)
                                                        • 1/2 teaspoon shahi jeera
                                                        • 1 tablespoon fennel seeds
                                                        • 1 medium onion, chopped
                                                        • 2 tablespoons ginger garlic paste
                                                        • 1 cup mint leaves
                                                        • 1 tablespoon kasuri methi
                                                        • 1 cup chopped tomato
                                                        • 1/2 kg chicken
                                                        • salt as needed
                                                        • 1 tablespoon garam masala powder

                                                        Rice

                                                        • 2 cups sona masoori rice
                                                        • 3.5 cups water
                                                        • 1 tablespoon lemon juice

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. Grind the coriander leaves and green chillies into a paste.
                                                        2. In a pan with oil, cook the whole spices briefly.
                                                        3. Add the chopped onion and fry until golden brown.
                                                        4. Add the ginger garlic paste and cook for about 2 minutes, then add the mint leaves and kasuri methi.
                                                        5. Add the chopped tomato and cook until the tomatoes are mushy.
                                                        6. Add the paste together with the chicken, salt as needed and the garam masala powder. Close the lid and cook the chicken for 10 minutes.
                                                        7. Once the chicken is cooked, add water as needed for the rice (2 cups of sona masoori rice needs 3.5 cups of water).
                                                        8. Add the lemon juice at the end and either keep it on dum at low flame for 20 minutes, or pressure cook for two whistles.
                                                        9. Serve hot with raita.

                                                        Chai Tiramisu with Cake Rusks

                                                        Transcribed from reel

                                                        A Diwali dessert crossing tiramisu, tres leches, and chai — cake rusks soaked in extra-strong chai and layered with a saffron cream, inspired by @dish_by_rish's eggless chai tiramisu idea.

                                                        Chai Soak

                                                        • 10 tea bags
                                                        • 30g ginger
                                                        • 15 cardamom pods
                                                        • 1/2 tsp black pepper
                                                        • 4 cloves
                                                        • 1 cinnamon stick
                                                        • 1 litre water (reduced by half during cooking)
                                                        • 150ml evaporated milk
                                                        • 75g white sugar

                                                        Saffron Cream

                                                        • 250g mascarpone cheese
                                                        • 250g cream cheese (or mascarpone)
                                                        • 4 eggs
                                                        • 100g granulated sugar
                                                        • A large pinch saffron
                                                        • 2 ice cubes

                                                        Assembly

                                                        • 24 cake rusks or tiramisu lady fingers
                                                        • 100g pistachios, finely chopped

                                                        Steps

                                                        1. First make the extra strong chai: bash up the spices in a pestle and mortar just until crushed. Add to a large pot with the tea bags and 1 litre of water. Cook on high for 20 mins with the lid off; it should reduce by half.
                                                        2. Add the evaporated milk and sugar and continue cooking for 10 mins. Allow to cool completely in the pan and then strain — you want about 600ml of liquid; top up with more evaporated milk or water if needed.
                                                        3. Meanwhile, grind the saffron with 1 tsp of the sugar, add a couple of ice cubes and let melt at room temperature.
                                                        4. Separate the whites and the yolks. In a clean bowl whisk the whites with half the sugar until you get stiff peaks.
                                                        5. Whisk the yolks with the remaining sugar until pale and thick — to the ribbon stage. Add in the mascarpone and cream cheese and continue to beat until thick and creamy. Add in the melted saffron and mix well.

                                                        Steps in the caption are truncated (it cuts off at "Using a spatula fold in half of the egg w…"), so the remaining assembly steps are not included; ingredients are transcribed in full.

                                                        Unbelievable 10-Minute Chocolate Pudding

                                                        From the creator's website: https://bakewithzoha.com/unbelievable-ten-minute-chocolate-pudding/

                                                        One pan, no mixer, no eggs — a rich, decadent, creamy chocolate pudding with a molten-chocolate-silk texture. Makes 4 servings.

                                                        Ingredients

                                                          Steps

                                                          1. Add all ingredients to a large nonstick saucepan.
                                                          2. Place on low heat and whisk gently; cook until the butter and chocolate melt and everything combines (it looks strange and lumpy at first but comes together as it cooks).
                                                          3. Keep cooking and stirring on low until the pudding comes to a boil; it thickens quickly after this point. Cook to a thick custard-like consistency (thick but still pourable), about 8–10 minutes total. Remove from the heat.
                                                          4. Strain the pudding to remove any lumps; pour into serving cups or a dish.
                                                          5. Serve warm right away, or refrigerate 2–4 hours for a chilled semi-set pudding.
                                                          6. Optionally garnish with fresh berries and/or chocolate shavings. Serve and enjoy.

                                                          Use good-quality chocolate bars or callets (not chips). Use the higher end of the chocolate range for a richer pudding. Keep the heat low and stir constantly — the cornstarch thickens fast once it simmers.

                                                            Madras Egg Puffs (Mutta Pupps)

                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                            Bakery-style Madras egg puffs: masala-coated halved eggs tucked into puff pastry and baked golden. Preheat oven or air fryer to 400F (200C).

                                                            Ingredients

                                                              Ingredients

                                                                Steps

                                                                1. Make the masala: saute the cumin seeds and onion in oil; add the chili, ginger-garlic paste and tomato; add the spices and salt; cook until dry; mix in the chopped coriander and gently coat the halved eggs in the masala.
                                                                2. Preheat the oven or air fryer to 400F (200C). Cut the pastry into 4 squares and place the masala plus half an egg in each.
                                                                3. Fold the corners, seal the edges and bake for 15-20 minutes until golden (optional: brush with egg wash or milk).

                                                                            Butter Chicken (No Butter/Cream Version)

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            A lighter butter chicken with no butter or cream — richness comes from cashews, milk, and chicken drippings.

                                                                            Marinade

                                                                            • 1/2 kg chicken thighs plus 4-5 drumsticks
                                                                            • 100 g Greek yogurt
                                                                            • 2 tbsp ginger-garlic paste
                                                                            • 1/2 lemon
                                                                            • 1/2 tbsp mustard oil
                                                                            • 1 tbsp dhaniya (coriander) powder
                                                                            • 1 tbsp garam masala
                                                                            • 1 tbsp chicken masala
                                                                            • 1 tbsp kasuri methi
                                                                            • 1/2 tbsp degi mirch
                                                                            • Salt

                                                                            Gravy

                                                                            • 1 onion
                                                                            • 4-5 garlic cloves
                                                                            • Ginger
                                                                            • 1 green chilli
                                                                            • 8 cashews
                                                                            • 1 bay leaf
                                                                            • 1 small cinnamon stick
                                                                            • 2-3 green cardamom
                                                                            • 2-4 cloves
                                                                            • 6-7 peppercorns
                                                                            • 1/2 cup milk
                                                                            • 500 g tomato puree
                                                                            • Water and extra milk, for consistency
                                                                            • Coriander, kasuri methi, and lemon, to garnish

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Marinate the chicken thighs and drumsticks with the Greek yogurt, ginger-garlic paste, lemon, mustard oil, and all the marinade spices.
                                                                            2. Cook the marinated chicken (the creator air-fries it).
                                                                            3. Deglaze the air-fryer basket drippings with warm water at the lowest temperature for 2-4 minutes and reserve to add to the gravy.
                                                                            4. In a nonstick pan, cook the onion, garlic, ginger, green chilli, cashews, and whole spices; add the tomato puree and cook low and slow, up to 30 minutes, until the tomatoes are really well cooked.
                                                                            5. Add the reserved chicken drippings to the gravy and adjust the consistency with water and milk.
                                                                            6. Finish with coriander, kasuri methi, and a squeeze of lemon.

                                                                            Caption gives the full ingredient set plus these key cooking tips; the reel video shows the complete cook-through.

                                                                            Prawn Pepper Fry

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            Big prawns tossed in a freshly roasted and ground pepper masala with curry leaves and lime.

                                                                            Prawns & Marinade

                                                                            • 15-20 big prawns
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp turmeric
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp Kashmiri chilli powder
                                                                            • Salt

                                                                            Pepper Masala (roast & grind)

                                                                            • 1 tbsp fennel seeds
                                                                            • 1 tbsp cumin seeds
                                                                            • 1 tbsp peppercorns
                                                                            • A few curry leaves

                                                                            Tempering & Sauce

                                                                            • Mustard oil
                                                                            • 1-inch cinnamon stick
                                                                            • 2 cardamom pods
                                                                            • 2 chopped onions
                                                                            • 1 tbsp ginger-garlic paste
                                                                            • Green chillies, chopped
                                                                            • 1 lime
                                                                            • Salt

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Marinate the prawns with salt, turmeric, and Kashmiri chilli powder.
                                                                            2. Dry-roast the fennel seeds, cumin seeds, peppercorns, and curry leaves, then grind into a pepper masala.
                                                                            3. Temper mustard oil with the cinnamon, cardamom, and other whole spices.
                                                                            4. Saute the onions and ginger-garlic paste, then add the pepper masala and marinated prawns.
                                                                            5. Cook covered for 3 minutes, then uncover; add the remaining masala, green chillies, salt, and curry leaves.
                                                                            6. Cook a few more minutes and squeeze lime over before serving.

                                                                            Indian-Style Cheesy Grilled Bread (Cheese Toast Bake)

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            Cheese Topping

                                                                            • 5 slices sourdough
                                                                            • 1/2 cup small-curd cottage cheese
                                                                            • 1 cup shredded Mexican cheese blend
                                                                            • 1 1/2 tomatoes, diced
                                                                            • 3/4 red onion, diced

                                                                            Perfect Chocolate Chip Cookies

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            Thick, chewy chocolate chip cookies finished with flaky sea salt — makes about 24 cookies.

                                                                            Cookie Dough

                                                                            • 1 cup butter
                                                                            • 1 cup brown sugar
                                                                            • 1/2 cup sugar
                                                                            • Vanilla
                                                                            • 1 egg
                                                                            • 2 1/4 cups flour
                                                                            • 1 tsp baking soda
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp salt
                                                                            • 2 cups mini chocolate chips
                                                                            • Flaky sea salt, for finishing

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Preheat the oven to 350°F.
                                                                            2. Cream the butter and both sugars together for 4-5 minutes.
                                                                            3. Add the vanilla and egg and mix to combine.
                                                                            4. Mix in the flour, baking soda, and salt, then fold in the mini chocolate chips.
                                                                            5. Scoop into 1/6-cup balls and top each with 8-10 regular chocolate chips.
                                                                            6. Bake for 9 minutes, finish with flaky sea salt, and rest on the pan for 9 minutes.

                                                                            Makes about 24 cookies.

                                                                            Caramelized Onion Pasta

                                                                            Recipe from reel caption

                                                                            A viral pasta built on deeply caramelized onions blended into a silky parmesan sauce — patience with the onions is the whole trick.

                                                                            Pasta & sauce

                                                                            • 4 large onions, sliced
                                                                            • ½ cup extra virgin olive oil
                                                                            • 4 tbsp butter
                                                                            • 3 egg yolks
                                                                            • 1 cup water
                                                                            • ¾ cup grated parmesan cheese
                                                                            • Salt & pepper, to taste
                                                                            • Cooked spaghetti
                                                                            • Fresh parsley, chopped (optional)

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Slice the onions and cook in the butter over medium heat until deeply caramelized, about 40 minutes (use a shortcut method if you can't wait).
                                                                            2. In a blender, combine the caramelized onions, olive oil, egg yolks, grated parmesan, water, salt and pepper; blend until smooth. Add the water in parts so the sauce doesn't get runny; thin with a little more water if needed.
                                                                            3. Toss the cooked spaghetti with the sauce in a large pan and warm over low heat for about 1 minute, then turn off the heat.
                                                                            4. Serve garnished with fresh parsley if desired.
