                                                                            Chocolate Cake with Chocolate Ganache

                                                                            Recipe from reel caption

                                                                            A medium three-layer chocolate cake with a silky chocolate ganache — rich, moist, and finished with a deliberately messy exterior.

                                                                            Cake

                                                                            • 1½ cups (225g) all-purpose flour
                                                                            • ¾ cup (150g) caster sugar
                                                                            • ⅔ cup (92g) cocoa powder
                                                                            • 1½ tsp baking soda
                                                                            • ⅔ tsp baking powder
                                                                            • ⅔ tsp salt
                                                                            • 2 eggs
                                                                            • ⅔ cup (165ml) buttermilk (or regular milk + 1 tsp white vinegar, rested 5 mins)
                                                                            • ⅔ cup (165ml) hot coffee
                                                                            • ¼ cup (62ml) vegetable oil
                                                                            • Vanilla paste, to taste

                                                                            Chocolate ganache

                                                                            • ½ cup dark chocolate
                                                                            • ½ cup heavy cream

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Preheat the oven to 175°C and line 7-inch cake tins with baking paper.
                                                                            2. Combine the flour, sugar, cocoa powder, baking soda, baking powder and salt in a bowl.
                                                                            3. In a separate bowl, combine the eggs, buttermilk, hot coffee, oil and vanilla and mix well.
                                                                            4. Combine the dry and wet ingredients, taking care not to over-mix.
                                                                            5. Divide between the three tins and bake 25-30 minutes, until a skewer comes out clean. Let the layers cool.
                                                                            6. For the ganache, melt the dark chocolate and heavy cream together and stir until smooth; let cool until spreadable.
                                                                            7. Assemble the cake, filling and coating with ganache — a messy exterior is the look here, no need for perfect smoothness.

                                                                            Makes a medium three-layer cake using 7-inch tins.

                                                                            View reel

                                                                            (High Protein) Italian Wedding Soup

                                                                            https://abrightmoment.com/high-protein-italian-wedding-soup/

                                                                            A high-protein take on the cozy classic: seared mini meatballs simmered with orzo, spinach and chicken bone broth. Prep 20 minutes, cook 20 minutes, serves 8.

                                                                            Meatballs

                                                                            • 1 lb ground beef
                                                                            • 2 Italian pork sausage links, casings removed
                                                                            • 3/4 cup panko breadcrumbs
                                                                            • 2 eggs
                                                                            • 1/2 cup grated parmesan
                                                                            • 1/4 cup fresh parsley
                                                                            • 1 tsp each garlic powder, salt & black pepper

                                                                            Soup

                                                                            • 4 tbsp olive oil
                                                                            • 1/2 yellow onion, chopped
                                                                            • 3 celery stalks, finely chopped
                                                                            • 3 carrots, finely chopped
                                                                            • 6 garlic cloves, minced or sliced
                                                                            • 1/3 cup white wine
                                                                            • 6 cups chicken bone broth
                                                                            • 3/4 cup orzo pasta, uncooked
                                                                            • 2 cups spinach
                                                                            • 2 bay leaves (optional)

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. In a large bowl, combine the ground beef, sausage, breadcrumbs, eggs, parmesan, parsley, garlic powder, salt and black pepper; mix until very well combined.
                                                                            2. Roll about one tablespoon of the mixture per meatball in the palms of your hands.
                                                                            3. In a large skillet over medium-high heat with a generous drizzle of olive oil, sear the mini meatballs until browned on all sides; set aside.
                                                                            4. In a large soup pot over medium-high heat with another generous drizzle of olive oil, add the onion, carrot, celery and garlic; sauté 4–5 minutes until fragrant and tender.
                                                                            5. Stir in the uncooked orzo pasta.
                                                                            6. Deglaze the pot with the white wine, stirring until evaporated.
                                                                            7. Add the chicken broth, bay leaves (if using), mini meatballs and spinach. Bring to a boil, then simmer 10 minutes.
                                                                            8. Serve warm with freshly grated parmesan on top.
                                                                            View reel

                                                                            Crockpot Chicken and Gravy

                                                                            https://www.thereciperebel.com/crockpot-chicken-and-gravy/

                                                                            A cozy family-favorite dinner with fall-apart tender chicken and real homemade gravy — no condensed soups or gravy packets. Ten minutes of prep, then the slow cooker does the rest.

                                                                            Ingredients

                                                                            • 1 lb boneless, skinless chicken breasts
                                                                            • 1 lb boneless, skinless chicken thighs
                                                                            • 1 1/2 cups low-sodium chicken broth
                                                                            • 2 tbsp corn starch
                                                                            • 1 tsp salt
                                                                            • 1 tsp Italian seasoning
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp onion powder
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp garlic powder
                                                                            • 1/4 tsp black pepper
                                                                            • 1/4 cup heavy whipping cream (optional)

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Place the chicken breasts and thighs in a 3–6 quart slow cooker.
                                                                            2. In a large glass measuring cup, whisk together the broth, corn starch, salt, Italian seasoning, onion powder, garlic powder and pepper until the corn starch is completely dissolved. Pour over the chicken.
                                                                            3. Cover and cook on low for about 3 hours, until a meat thermometer in the thickest piece reads 165°F (time varies with slow cooker size and fullness).
                                                                            4. Remove the chicken to a cutting board and pull or shred into pieces.
                                                                            5. Stir the gravy to combine (strain through a fine mesh sieve if desired for extra smoothness), then stir in the cream if using. Taste and adjust seasonings.
                                                                            6. Serve the chicken with the gravy spooned over — great with mashed potatoes or crusty bread.

                                                                            Leftovers keep 3–4 days refrigerated or up to 3 months frozen; reheat gently on the stovetop. Gravy too thin? Cook on high with the lid off a few minutes. Too thick? Whisk in a splash of broth or milk.

                                                                            View reel

                                                                            Dahi Tadka Chicken

                                                                            Transcribed from reel

                                                                            Crispy air-fried chicken folded into creamy Greek yogurt with a fragrant tadka of mustard seeds, curry leaves and green chillies. Finished with fresh coriander and onions; serve with roti or paratha.

                                                                            Chicken & Marinade

                                                                            • 400 g chicken breast, cut into pieces
                                                                            • 1 tbsp lime juice
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp salt
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp pepper
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp chilli powder
                                                                            • 1/4 tsp coriander powder
                                                                            • 1 tbsp ginger garlic paste

                                                                            Yogurt Base

                                                                            • 3/4 cup Greek yogurt

                                                                            Tadka

                                                                            • 1 tbsp ghee
                                                                            • 2 tsp mustard seeds
                                                                            • 1 sprig curry leaves
                                                                            • 2 green chillies, slit
                                                                            • 1/4 tsp red chilli powder
                                                                            • A pinch of turmeric powder
                                                                            • Salt & pepper, to taste

                                                                            To Serve

                                                                            • 1 small onion, sliced
                                                                            • Coriander, to garnish
                                                                            • Roti or paratha

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Marinate the chicken with the lime juice, salt, pepper, chilli powder, coriander powder and ginger garlic paste.
                                                                            2. Line a tray with parchment paper, arrange the marinated chicken pieces on it and drizzle with oil.
                                                                            3. Air fry at 230°C for about 15 minutes, until cooked through and crispy.
                                                                            4. In a pan, heat the ghee and add the mustard seeds, slit green chillies, curry leaves, red chilli powder and turmeric; cook until it starts crackling.
                                                                            5. Pour the hot tadka over the Greek yogurt and stir well.
                                                                            6. Add the air-fried chicken pieces, pour over the yogurt and mix everything well.
                                                                            7. Garnish with chopped coriander and sliced onions; serve with roti or paratha of choice.
                                                                            View reel

                                                                            Restaurant-Worthy Guacamole

                                                                            https://playswellwithbutter.com/best-guacamole-recipe-ever/

                                                                            Chunky-creamy guac with a secret ingredient: Tajín. Ready in 15 minutes; serves 6.

                                                                            Ingredients

                                                                            • 2 large ripe Hass avocados, halved and de-seeded
                                                                            • 1/4 medium red onion, finely diced (approx. 1/4 cup)
                                                                            • 2 jalapeño peppers, de-seeded as desired and finely diced
                                                                            • 2 cloves garlic, roughly chopped
                                                                            • 1 large juicy lime, juiced (approx. 3 tbsp)
                                                                            • 1/3 cup finely chopped cilantro
                                                                            • 1 tsp kosher salt
                                                                            • Heaping 1/2 tsp garlic powder
                                                                            • Heaping 1/2 tsp chile lime seasoning (such as Tajín)
                                                                            • For serving: finely chopped cilantro, tortilla chips, etc.

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Smash the aromatics: add the onion, jalapeño and garlic to a resealable bag with a pinch of salt. Seal, pressing out the air, and smash with a rolling pin or heavy pan until juicy and softened but not a paste. (Or use a molcajete.)
                                                                            2. Mix: scoop the avocado flesh into a large bowl; add the smashed vegetables and all remaining ingredients (lime juice through Tajín). Smash gently with a spoon to your desired consistency and stir to combine. Taste and adjust salt/lime.
                                                                            3. Serve immediately with tortilla chips, topped with extra cilantro and Tajín.

                                                                            Afghani Egg Curry

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            Gravy base

                                                                            • 2 tbsp oil
                                                                            • 1 big onion, sliced
                                                                            • 2 inches ginger
                                                                            • 8–9 garlic cloves
                                                                            • 8–10 cashews
                                                                            • 8–10 green chillies
                                                                            • Salt to taste
                                                                            • Water
                                                                            • Coriander leaves (to blend)

                                                                            Eggs

                                                                            • 4 boiled eggs, sliced
                                                                            • Red chilli powder, salt, turmeric (to roast)

                                                                            Tempering & finishing

                                                                            • Oil
                                                                            • 1 cinnamon stick
                                                                            • 2 cardamom
                                                                            • 3 cloves
                                                                            • Blended gravy
                                                                            • Curd
                                                                            • Black pepper powder
                                                                            • Roasted cumin powder
                                                                            • Garam masala
                                                                            • Turmeric
                                                                            • Salt
                                                                            • Kasuri methi
                                                                            • Cream
                                                                            • Water

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Cook the onion, ginger, garlic, cashews, green chillies, salt and water; cool completely, add coriander leaves and blend.

                                                                            Palakoora Ullikaram (Spinach–Onion Chutney)

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            Ingredients

                                                                            • 4 bunches spinach
                                                                            • 3 medium onions
                                                                            • 1 tbsp oil (first tempering)
                                                                            • 1 tsp chana dal
                                                                            • 2 tsp coriander seeds
                                                                            • 1 tsp cumin
                                                                            • 5–6 red chillies
                                                                            • Salt
                                                                            • Small piece of tamarind
                                                                            • 2 tsp red chilli powder
                                                                            • 2 tbsp oil (second tempering)
                                                                            • 1 tsp each cumin seeds, mustard seeds, chana dal
                                                                            • 1 tsp garlic

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. In a pan with 1 tbsp oil, fry the chana dal, coriander seeds, cumin and red chillies for 5 minutes; transfer to a mixer jar.
                                                                            2. In the same oil, fry the onions till golden brown; add salt and a small piece of tamarind. Turn off the flame and cool.
                                                                            3. Grind the fried spices to a coarse powder; add the onions and grind again with salt to taste and 2 tsp red chilli powder, coarsely.
                                                                            4. In another pan with 2 tbsp oil, add cumin seeds, mustard seeds, chana dal (1 tsp each) and garlic; temper.
                                                                            5. Add the spinach (and the ground mixture per the video) and cook through.

                                                                            Steps follow the caption; the final combining is demonstrated in the reel.

                                                                            Air Fried Chicken Roast

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            Marinade (1 kg chicken)

                                                                            • 1 kg chicken
                                                                            • Salt
                                                                            • 1.5 tbsp Kashmiri chilli powder
                                                                            • 1 tbsp coriander powder
                                                                            • 3/4 tsp garam masala
                                                                            • 1/2 tsp turmeric
                                                                            • 2 tbsp vinegar
                                                                            • 2 tbsp dark soy sauce
                                                                            • 2 tbsp ginger-garlic paste

                                                                            Roast masala

                                                                            • 2 tbsp oil
                                                                            • 1 cup sliced shallots
                                                                            • 1/2 tbsp Kashmiri chilli powder
                                                                            • 1/2 tbsp coriander powder
                                                                            • 2 tbsp ketchup
                                                                            • 2 green chillies
                                                                            • 1/2 cup hot water

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Marinate the chicken with all marinade ingredients for 1 hour.
                                                                            2. Spray with oil and air fry 25 minutes.
                                                                            3. In a pan, heat the oil and sauté the shallots until translucent.
                                                                            4. Add the chilli powder, coriander powder, ketchup and green chillies; sauté a while.
                                                                            5. Add the air-fried chicken; sauté, then add the hot water and sauté a few more minutes.

                                                                            Crispy Baked Chicken with Roasted Pepper Sauce

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            Makes 2 servings (467 kcal, 50 g protein per serving); sauce makes about 4 servings (140 kcal).

                                                                            Chicken

                                                                            • 450 g (about 4 pieces) boneless skinless chicken thigh

                                                                            Marinade

                                                                            • 1/2 cup curd/Greek yoghurt
                                                                            • 2 tsp garlic paste
                                                                            • 2 tsp peri peri seasoning
                                                                            • 1 tsp chilli flakes
                                                                            • 1 egg
                                                                            • Salt to taste

                                                                            Crumb

                                                                            • 1/2 cup panko bread crumbs
                                                                            • 2 tbsp cornflour
                                                                            • 1 tsp peri peri seasoning
                                                                            • 1 tsp chilli flakes
                                                                            • Salt to taste
                                                                            • Pinch of Aromat powder (optional)

                                                                            Sauce

                                                                            • 1/2 red bell pepper, lightly roasted
                                                                            • 3–4 garlic cloves (optionally lightly cooked)
                                                                            • 30 g paneer
                                                                            • 1/3 cup skim milk
                                                                            • 1 tbsp broken cashewnuts
                                                                            • 1 tsp peri peri seasoning
                                                                            • 1 tsp chilli flakes
                                                                            • Salt to taste

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Brine the chicken in salted water a few hours or overnight in the fridge.
                                                                            2. Whisk the marinade ingredients; marinate the chicken at least 1–2 hours.
                                                                            3. Mix the crumb ingredients; coat the chicken thighs on both sides.
                                                                            4. Place on a greased baking tray, brush with oil; bake at 200°C for 15 minutes, then switch to top heat/grill for 5 minutes. (Air fryer: 200°C for 10 minutes, flip, 10–12 minutes more.)
                                                                            5. Rest 5–10 minutes.
                                                                            6. Blend all sauce ingredients; season as needed.
                                                                            7. Serve the chicken with the sauce.

                                                                            Black Urad Dal Adai (Karupu Ulundu Adai)

                                                                            https://myflavourfactory.com/black-urad-dal-adai-karupu-ulundu-adai/

                                                                            No-ferment, gluten-free, vegan adai — light, crispy and high-protein. Serves 4.

                                                                            Ingredients

                                                                            • 1/2 cup black urad (same as used for dal makhni)
                                                                            • 1 cup parboiled rice (idli rice, ponni rice or sona masoori; do not use basmati)
                                                                            • 1 tsp pepper
                                                                            • 1 tsp cumin
                                                                            • 1 inch ginger
                                                                            • 1 tsp hing/asafoetida
                                                                            • Salt to taste
                                                                            • Water, to grind
                                                                            • 1 cup onion, finely chopped
                                                                            • Finely chopped curry leaves and coriander leaves

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Rinse and soak the black urad and rice together for 2 hours (no longer).
                                                                            2. Grind with ginger, pepper and cumin, then add hing and salt; use water as needed for a coarse yet spreadable batter.
                                                                            3. Stir in the chopped onions, curry leaves and coriander leaves.
                                                                            4. Make adais on a hot griddle using sesame oil (or a sesame oil–ghee mix), cooking until crisp on both sides.

                                                                            Crispy Roast Potatoes (Air Fryer)

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            • 5 Maris Piper potatoes
                                                                            • A sprig of thyme
                                                                            • 6 garlic cloves
                                                                            • 2–3 tbsp oil
                                                                            • Salt

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Peel the potatoes and cut into odd shapes.

                                                                            Makhni Chicken (Pakistani-Style Butter Chicken)

                                                                            https://rookiewithacookie.com/makhni-chicken-its-not-butter-chicken/

                                                                            Creamy, delicately spiced makhni with no sweet undertones and no onions. Serves 4.

                                                                            Ingredients

                                                                            • 1/3 cup oil
                                                                            • 1 lb chicken thighs, cubed
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon salt
                                                                            • 2 teaspoon ginger garlic paste
                                                                            • 3/4 teaspoon cumin powder
                                                                            • 1/4 teaspoon coriander powder
                                                                            • 1/8 teaspoon garam masala
                                                                            • 1/4 teaspoon red chili powder
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon red chili flakes
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon black pepper, ground
                                                                            • 1/4 cup yogurt, lightly beaten
                                                                            • 3 tomatoes, pureed (from a can of whole peeled tomatoes)
                                                                            • 1/2 cup heavy cream
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon black pepper
                                                                            • 2 tablespoon butter
                                                                            • 1 tablespoon dried fenugreek leaves (kasuri methi)

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Fry the chicken in the oil; once the color changes, add salt and ginger garlic paste.
                                                                            2. Add the spices and fry 3–4 minutes to coat the chicken; add the yogurt and mix for another two minutes.
                                                                            3. Puree the tomatoes and pour into the pan. Bring to a boil, then cook covered on medium heat for 15 minutes.
                                                                            4. Remove the lid and cook on high heat ("bhoon") until the water evaporates and the oil separates.
                                                                            5. Lower to medium-low; add the heavy cream, black pepper and a few slit green chillies. Cover and simmer 2–3 minutes.
                                                                            6. Finish with butter and kasuri methi. Serve hot with naan.
                                                                            View reel

                                                                            One-Dish Butter Chicken Pie

                                                                            Transcribed from reel caption

                                                                            510 Cal | 53g protein | 30g carbs | 19g fat per pie. Serves 4, made in 2 1/2-cup (600 ml) glass containers.

                                                                            Ingredients

                                                                            • 1 3/4 lb (780 g) chicken breast, diced into small pieces
                                                                            • 7 oz (200 g) puff pastry (1 serving per pie)

                                                                            Butter Chicken Base Sauce

                                                                            • 1 1/2 cups protein boosted milk (Vitasoy)
                                                                            • 3/4 cup (250 g) Greek yogurt
                                                                            • 6 tbsp (170 g) tomato paste

                                                                            Butter Chicken Sauce Spices

                                                                            • Use your favorite packet of butter chicken paste, or the homemade mix: 2 tbsp (12 g) curry powder, 1 tbsp (6 g) garam masala, 1 tbsp (6 g) ground cumin, 2 tsp ground ginger, 2 tsp garlic powder, 1.5 tsp salt, 1 tsp turmeric

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Preheat oven to 400°F (200°C).
                                                                            2. In a large bowl, whisk together the butter chicken base sauce and spices.
                                                                            3. Add diced raw chicken to oven-safe containers; divide the milk mixture evenly among them.
                                                                            4. Top each container with a square of puff pastry; cut a small slit in the top for steam. Brush pastry with beaten egg.
                                                                            5. Place containers on a baking sheet and bake 25–30 minutes, until the pastry is golden brown and the filling is bubbling (chicken internal temp 165°F/74°C).
                                                                            6. Let stand 5 minutes before serving. Caution: containers will be hot.

                                                                            Marbled Chocolate Chip Cookies

                                                                            https://handletheheat.com/marbled-chocolate-chip-cookies/

                                                                            Classic brown sugar chocolate chip cookies and double chocolate chip cookies swirled together.

                                                                            For the base

                                                                            • 2 sticks (227 g) unsalted butter, at cool room temperature
                                                                            • 1/2 cup (100 g) granulated sugar
                                                                            • 1 1/4 cups (247 g) lightly packed light brown sugar
                                                                            • 2 teaspoons vanilla
                                                                            • 2 large eggs, at room temperature

                                                                            For the chocolate chip dough

                                                                            • 1 1/2 cups (190 g) all-purpose flour
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon baking soda
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon fine sea salt
                                                                            • 3/4 cup (128 g) semisweet chocolate wafers or feves

                                                                            For the cocoa dough

                                                                            • 1 1/4 cups plus 2 tablespoons (175 g) all-purpose flour
                                                                            • 1/4 cup (21 g) unsweetened natural cocoa powder
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon baking soda
                                                                            • 1/2 teaspoon fine sea salt
                                                                            • 1/4 teaspoon instant espresso powder, optional
                                                                            • 3/4 cup (128 g) semisweet chocolate wafers or feves
                                                                            • Flaky sea salt, for finishing, if desired

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Preheat oven to 350°F. Line baking sheets with parchment paper.
                                                                            2. Beat the butter, granulated sugar and brown sugar until creamy, about 3 minutes. Add vanilla and eggs; beat until combined.
                                                                            3. Whisk the chocolate chip dough dry ingredients; add half the creamed butter-sugar mixture (about 330 g) and stir into a cohesive dough. Mix in the chocolate wafers, reserving some for garnish.
                                                                            4. To the remaining butter-sugar mixture add the cocoa dough dry ingredients; beat on low to combine. Mix in the chocolate wafers, reserving some for garnish.
                                                                            5. Scoop portions of both doughs with a 1 1/2-tablespoon scoop. Tear one ball in half and sandwich the other dough ball inside (or vice versa); roll between palms. Place on the baking sheet and dot with reserved wafers.
                                                                            6. If time permits, refrigerate the shaped dough 24–72 hours for thicker, chewier, more flavorful cookies.
                                                                            7. Bake 11–13 minutes, until golden at the edges. Sprinkle with flaky sea salt if desired. Cool 5 minutes, then transfer to wire racks.
                                                                            View reel

                                                                            Moelleux au Chocolat (Melt in the Middle Chocolate Cake)

                                                                            https://desertislanddishes.co/moelleux-au-chocolat/

                                                                            A giant melt-in-the-middle chocolate cake — rich, gooey and decadent, especially served warm. Naturally gluten free, just 5 ingredients.

                                                                            For the cake

                                                                            • 1 cup unsalted butter (2 sticks), cut into cubes, plus extra for greasing
                                                                            • 8 oz good-quality 70% dark chocolate, roughly chopped (about 1 cup)
                                                                            • 2.6 oz cocoa powder (about 3/4 cup)
                                                                            • Pinch of salt
                                                                            • 7 large eggs
                                                                            • 9.3 oz superfine (caster) sugar (about 1 1/3 cups), divided

                                                                            To serve

                                                                            • Heavy (double) cream, or ice cream, or custard

                                                                            Steps

                                                                            1. Preheat the oven to 400°F (200°C/180°C fan). Generously butter a 9-inch (23 cm) round springform pan and line the base with parchment paper.
                                                                            2. Melt the butter and chocolate together in a heatproof bowl over simmering water (or microwave in 30-second bursts), stirring until smooth.
                                                                            3. Sift in the cocoa powder and salt; stir until smooth. Set aside.
                                                                            4. Separate the eggs. Add about 4 3/4 oz (135 g) of the sugar to the yolks and whisk until pale, light and thick.
                                                                            5. Whisk the egg whites to soft peaks, gradually adding the remaining sugar, until stiff and glossy.
                                                                            6. Pour the melted chocolate mixture into the egg yolks and stir gently. Stir in 2 spoonfuls of whisked egg whites vigorously (the "sacrificial" spoon), then gently fold in the remaining whites.
                                                                            7. Pour 2/3 of the batter into the prepared pan and bake about 25 minutes, until well risen and a skewer comes out clean. Leave to cool completely in the pan (it may collapse — that is normal).
                                                                            8. Keep the remaining 1/3 of batter in the fridge.
                                                                            9. Once the cake is cool, remove from the pan, place on a lined baking sheet and spread the reserved batter on top.
                                                                            10. Bake again 10–15 minutes, until a thin crust forms but the middle is still soft and jiggly. Rest 10 minutes.
                                                                            11. Slice and serve warm with cream, ice cream or custard (or chilled from the fridge).
                                                                            View reel
